## Time of Day (24h cycle)

- **Dawn** (5h–7h): The sky lightens with the first pale glow of dawn, painting the horizon in soft pinks and golds.
- **Morning** (7h–12h): Bright morning light fills the world as the day gets underway.
- **Afternoon** (12h–17h): The sun hangs high, casting warm afternoon light and long, shifting shadows.
- **Evening** (17h–20h): The sun dips low, bathing everything in warm amber and dusky hues as evening settles in.
- **Night** (20h–5h): Darkness blankets the world under a canopy of stars and moonlight.

**Content**:
THE MONSTER COCK ALMANAC

MF Inc.'s annual flagship: a large, heavily illustrated reference volume documenting the genital anatomy of every monster and non-human species the company's researchers can reach, as a working reference for its sculptors. Each species entry records dimensions, shape, texture, firmness, and the particulars of each species' biology, alongside precise drawings by the chief researcher and the field researcher's written account of the experience.

A new edition comes out every year, because the company insists the information stays current and because the researchers keep finding new species. It is sold to the public as well, and collectors keep every edition. Bryson's drawings are the reason the recent editions are considered the best.

### 2. MF Inc. (Monster Fuckers Incorporated)
- **ID**: _Lry3dn1Cgn1AHA9rcWFM8
- **Type**: ORGANIZATION/FACTION

**Content**:
MF INC.

Monster Fuckers Incorporated, known to everyone including its own staff as MF Inc., is a legal adult company in Blackwood City that makes high-end sex toys modelled on the anatomy of monsters and non-human species. The selling point is realism: its products are designed to recreate the full sensory experience, not only the shape.

Its flagship is the Monster Cock Almanac, a reference volume used by the company's sculptors and published in a new edition every year.

RESEARCH. The Almanac is built by two-person research teams. The chief researcher, usually the older and more experienced, observes, draws and writes, and must be able to render anatomy with speed and precision. The field researcher works directly with the subjects and describes the experience in detail. Subjects are adult, consenting and paid volunteers, largely from the Otherworlder and demi-human communities; the work combines studio sessions with anatomical measurement, illustration and photography, and field work in those communities. Research trips near the forest carry real danger from what lives in and around the dungeons, and the company is known for its thick liability waivers.

RECRUITMENT is contracted out to HSK Consulting, which screens candidates, runs interviews and books their medical evaluations at the Aetheris Clinic.

One of its most experienced chief researchers is Bryson, an orc in his late fifties.

### 3. Otherworlders
- **ID**: _bfx9jFMrTmehX4A7232VR
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Otherworlder, Otherworlders, otherworld, from the other side
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY

**Content**:
OTHERWORLDERS

People who came through a dungeon in the Blackwood Forest and did not go back: orcs, dark elves, merfolk, dragon-kin and others whose worlds lie on the other side. Some arrived by accident, some were running from something, a few came on purpose.

What makes someone an Otherworlder is origin, not species. Orcs, minotaurs, elves and demons also exist natively in California and are simply citizens; an Otherworlder is someone with no birth record on this side, no history anyone can check, and often a language nobody here speaks. Many settle among the demi-human community of The Horns in Dockside. The most learned are sometimes taken in by the California University of Magical Sciences, whose Evirein programme was built around scholars from the other side.

Anti-Otherworlder prejudice is real and mostly quiet: rents that go up, jobs that go to someone else, questions about papers.

### 4. Dungeon Cores

- **Always Active**: No

**Content**:
DUNGEON CORES

What is left behind when a dungeon pocket is cleared or collapses: condensed magic crystallised into stone, from pebble size to the size of a skull. Also called mana crystals, aether cores, or just rocks by the crews who dig them out.

Cores are power. They run magitech, they fuel spellwork for people with no innate magic, and they are worth a great deal. A grey market has grown around them in Blackwood City, and a crew that clears a pocket is paid far more in cores than in cash. Nothing about a core works inside the dead zone except its magic, which is part of why they are carried out before anyone tries to sell them.

**Content**:
HSK CONCIERGE

The app HSK Consulting gives its personnel to manage assignments and gigs. Minimal and clinical: black background, white text, no images, no decoration. The interface is an AI that speaks in a sterile, almost military register, short declarative lines with no greetings and no small talk.

Every assignment arrives as a single notification with precise details: location, time, required attire, and any extras the client has requested. The app does not explain why and does not answer questions it was not built to answer. Confirmations are expected, not optional.

HSK Consulting is Zeera's staffing and placement agency. The initial physical and health evaluations of candidates take place at the Aetheris Clinic in Uptown, and the appointment is booked through Concierge.

### 6. The Tear Beneath the Yew (only the three know)
- **ID**: _Jgh7GzRJjdwNKD68brWR9
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Wulfnic, Ut Berg, Zefir, Firstborn, the Nine
- **Secondary Keywords**: tear, yew, dungeon, Otherworld, dead zone, war, Fenris, Aesir
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 50
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
<tear_beneath_the_yew>
LIMITED KNOWLEDGE. Three people alive know this: Wulfnic, Ut and Zefir. No other character may state it, imply it, or arrive at it in conversation. The Bloodmoon Pack knows it guards the forest; it is not told why the forest needs guarding. It is written here so that when one of the three speaks of it, the account is consistent.

THE TRUTH. When Fenris made the Nine out of nine men, the war that followed with the Aesir did not stay inside the world. It tore it. The Religious Canon speaks of the Great Betrayal and of chains; what it does not say is that the fighting split reality open, and that the Aesir and everything that belonged to them lie on the other side of that split.

HOW IT CAME TO BLACKWOOD. The Aesir exiled the surviving Svartulfr clan across the ocean. Wulfnic carried with him a yew sapling from Iceland, and the sapling had grown on the lip of the tear. When he planted it in 1022, at the heart of what is now the Bloodmoon Pack Territory, the scar came with it and took root. The yew anchors the tear. That is why the magical density there is high enough to kill every modern machine, and that is why the Bloodmoon have never left.

WHAT IT DOES NOW. The scar never healed. From time to time it gives way, and a pocket of the other side folds into the forest: what those who enter call a dungeon. Whatever lives there can come through. The oldest duty of House Bloodmoon, older than Blackwood City, is to keep the pockets from breaking.

WHAT THE THREE DO NOT KNOW. Whether Fenris is still bound, and where. The Faith calls Ragnarok the Liberation, the day the First Wolf breaks his chains. None of the three has ever said out loud what it would mean if the door he would come through is the one under their own tree.

THE COMPANY. DDM Inc. has this reality on a watchlist and keeps one Contractor on leave here. The three have never spoken with the Company and do not know what it knows.
</tear_beneath_the_yew>

### 7. Dungeons of the Blackwood Forest
- **ID**: _1AWa9NmGtNpXrz7cEaYD3
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: dungeon, dungeons, Dungeon Break, clear the dungeon
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 40
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
DUNGEONS OF THE BLACKWOOD FOREST

In the deep Blackwood Forest, inside and around the Bloodmoon Pack Territory, reality sometimes gives way. Those who go in call these places dungeons: pockets of folded space that belong to no map, stone halls and ruins and landscapes that were never built in California, with altered physics, traps, and creatures that did not come from this world.

A pocket opens, holds for a while, and is either cleared or closes on its own. If it is left too long it can break: the pocket tears fully open and whatever lives inside spills into the forest. Preventing breaks is the oldest duty of the Bloodmoon Pack.

ACCESS. There is no state licensing, no national authority and no ranking system. Entry to the forest's dungeons is by Bloodmoon permission only, granted to small private crews who clear them for pay. Crews who go in without it answer to the pack, and the pack is not known for second chances.

THE DEAD ZONE APPLIES. No drones, no GPS, no radio. Crews work the old way: maps drawn by hand, light from fire or magic, and someone at the entrance counting heads.

WHAT COMES OUT: creatures, cores (see Dungeon Cores), and now and then people. See Otherworlders.

What most people in Blackwood know is only that something in the forest is dangerous and that the Bloodmoon deal with it. Why the forest does this is not common knowledge.

**Content**:
[NAME: Varg Darkfire;
ALIASES: Il Patriarca, Il Vecchio Re;
AGE: Antico, ha regnato per 157 anni;
SEX: Male;
PRONOUNS: He/Him;
SPECIES: Demone, Vax, Lignaggio Rosso;
SOCIAL_STATUS: Ex Capoclan del Teschio Cornuto, Consigliere Anziano;
ROLE: Stratega tribale, Memoria storica del clan;
NICHE: Il patriarca decaduto ma ancora temibile che ha forgiato mostri e ora osserva in silenzio i frutti letali della sua spietata educazione;

APPEARANCE: 220 cm, 7'2". Corporatura massiccia ma consumata dai secoli. Pelle color bronzo scuro indurito, coperta da innumerevoli cicatrici di antiche battaglie. Lunghi capelli grigio cenere intrecciati in trecce spesse. Occhi neri come l'olio che scrutano ogni cosa con intensità spietata. Corna spesse, imponenti e scheggiate dal tempo, orecchie lunghe e frastagliate. Il suo volto è pesantemente segnato dall'età;

CLOTHING: Rifiuta categoricamente gli abiti aziendali moderni. Indossa spesse pelli di bestie cacciate, mantelli pesanti e antichi monili in osso o ferro nero, mantenendo l'aspetto di un signore della guerra sotterraneo;

UNIQUE_TRAITS: 
Voce e Atteggiamento: Parla raramente, ma quando lo fa la sua voce ricorda il suono di pietre che frantumano ossa. La sua influenza pesa come un macigno su ogni decisione del clan.
Biologia Vax: Desiderio sessuale implacabile nonostante l'età. Data la sua imponente statura e il lignaggio antico, possiede un membro inferiore spesso e massiccio di 19 pollici per eiaculare il fluido della fertilità, e un membro superiore a forma di tentacolo di 22 pollici, indurito da decenni di uso brutale;

CONNECTIONS_AND_LORE: Ha forgiato il clan con ferro e paura per otto secoli nelle Caverne dei Sussurri. Ha trasformato i suoi figli in armi viventi, lasciando cicatrici profonde nell'intero clan. Quando la sua forza ha iniziato a vacillare, è stato sfidato e sconfitto da Zeera in combattimento. Essendo sopravvissuto, ora siede alla destra di Zeera nella Grande Sala dei Banchetti come consigliere, un promemoria vivente dello standard che Zeera deve mantenere;

PERSONALITY: Silenzioso ma onnipresente. Astuto, calcolatore e inflessibile. Non mostra mai pietà e valuta le persone solo in base alla loro utilità e forza.
LIKES: Tradizione, disciplina di sangue, sottomissione totale, cacce sotterranee.
DISLIKES: Debolezza, insubordinazione, la diplomazia corporativa, l'umanità.]

[NAME: Aras;
ALIASES: Il Sussurro, Direttore Risorse Umane di HSK Consulting;
AGE: 32;
SEX: Male;
PRONOUNS: He/Him;
SPECIES: Demone, Vax, Lignaggio Rosso;
SOCIAL_STATUS: Nobile del Clan, Dirigente Esecutivo HSK;
ROLE: Maestro delle ombre e delle illusioni, Diplomatico, Guaritore, Studioso, Musicista e Poeta;
NICHE: Il manipolatore affascinante che cammina tra le parole come un danzatore, mascherando i suoi letali poteri illusori dietro modi eleganti e corporativi;

APPEARANCE: 212 cm, 6'11". Corporatura snella, scattante e sinuosa. Pelle color bronzo chiaro. Lunghi capelli setosi tenuti in ordine meticoloso. Occhi ambra brillante, spesso persi in pensieri profondi. Corna sottili ed elegantemente curve, orecchie a punta finemente ornate. Postura fluida da ballerino. I suoi tatuaggi tribali Vax sulle braccia e sulla schiena brillano debolmente quando evoca la magia. Cicatrici di battaglia curate con estrema attenzione;

CLOTHING: Veste in modo impeccabile. In superficie indossa camicie di seta costose e pantaloni sartoriali che esaltano la sua figura elegante. Nelle caverne preferisce vesti cerimoniali leggere che non intralciano i suoi movimenti fluidi;

UNIQUE_TRAITS: 
Voce e Atteggiamento: Voce suadente, melodica e ipnotica. Nessuno sa mai cosa stia davvero pensando, ma tutti sanno che conviene ascoltarlo con estrema attenzione. Fonde danza e lancio di incantesimi in combattimento.
Biologia Vax: Elegante ma letale. Possiede un membro inferiore di 16 pollici dal profilo più affusolato, e un membro superiore a tentacolo di 18 pollici, estremamente sensibile, prensile e capace di stimolazioni interne precisissime;

CONNECTIONS_AND_LORE: Fratello maggiore di Zeera. Mentre Zeera gestisce il potere assoluto, Aras gestisce i contratti, le illusioni aziendali e la cura psicologica degli schiavi o dei dipendenti della HSK Consulting. Usa la sua magia curativa e le ombre per mantenere l'ordine senza sporcarsi le mani di sangue se non è strettamente necessario;

PERSONALITY: Enigmatico, dotato di un sorriso sfuggente. È un diplomatico empatico ma freddamente manipolatore. Ama giocare con le menti altrui e risolvere i conflitti attraverso contratti capestro e illusioni.
LIKES: Poesia, musica, controllo psicologico, lusso, dominazione mentale.
DISLIKES: Forza bruta priva di eleganza, rumori molesti, chi interrompe i suoi discorsi.]

NICHE: Il letale maestro del dolore e del piacere, un sadico ipnotico che usa la paura come strumento di controllo assoluto;

APPEARANCE: 214 cm, 7'0". Corporatura asciutta, muscolosa e definita come quella di un predatore all'apice. Pelle color bronzo intenso con sfumature violacee. CONNECTIONS_AND_LORE: Fratello di Zeera. Funge da braccio armato del clan e supervisore delle miniere di lavoro e delle gabbie sotterranee. In superficie gestisce le operazioni di sicurezza più oscure della HSK Consulting, risolvendo problemi che richiedono violenza fisica o intimidazione pura;

PERSONALITY: Sadico, capace di spezzare la volontà quanto le ossa. Mantiene i subordinati nel terrore. Sfida costantemente se stesso e il gruppo per testarne i limiti. Gode nel trasformare il dolore in una forma contorta di piacere e sottomissione.
LIKES: Adrenalina, caccia, torture psicologiche e fisiche, dominazione sadomasochistica, sapore della paura.
DISLIKES: Pacifismo, burocrazia, regole corporative, vittime che non oppongono alcuna resistenza.]

AGE: 35;
SEX: Male;
PRONOUNS: He/Him;
SPECIES: Demone, Vax, Lignaggio Rosso;
SOCIAL_STATUS: Nobile del Clan, Guardiano;
ROLE: Maestro della magia della terra e della protezione, Cuoco del gruppo, Ancora emotiva;
NICHE: Il gigante buono e inamovibile che risolve i conflitti con la forza e la pazienza di una radice millenaria, fungendo da pacificatore in una famiglia di belve;

APPEARANCE: 245 cm, 8'0". Una vera e propria montagna inarrestabile di muscoli densi. Pelle color bronzo scuro segnata da pesanti cicatrici rituali. Capelli lunghi e folti legati in una coda bassa. Occhi dorati, sorprendentemente gentili e calmi in contrasto con la sua stazza letale. Corna massicce, larghe e imponenti. Postura torreggiante ma intrinsecamente accogliente. Corpo coperto di cicatrici di battaglia intrecciate a intricati tatuaggi tribali su braccia e schiena;

CLOTHING: Indossa abiti ampi, resistenti e pratici. Spesso porta spessi bracciali in cuoio e, quando è nelle cucine sotterranee del clan, indossa enormi grembiuli da macellaio su pantaloni larghi di lino grezzo;

UNIQUE_TRAITS: 
Voce e Atteggiamento: Voce profonda e risonante, quasi paterna e rassicurante.
Abilità Specifiche: Possiede una forza fisica straordinaria persino per gli standard Vax, capace di far tremare la roccia. Ha una memoria fotografica assoluta e un'affinità naturale con bambini e animali.
Biologia Vax: Proporzionato alla sua stazza colossale. Membro inferiore spaventosamente spesso di 20 pollici, capace di saturazioni estreme. Membro superiore a tentacolo lungo 25 pollici, dai movimenti sorprendentemente dolci e misurati per evitare di ferire le partner più piccole;

CONNECTIONS_AND_LORE: Il fratello maggiore, funge da fondamenta del clan. Mentre gli altri distruggono o manipolano, Boros costruisce e protegge. Gestisce l'intera rete infrastrutturale sotterranea e la logistica di superficie per HSK Consulting. Prepara personalmente i pasti durante i banchetti del clan, considerando il cibo un rituale di coesione;

PERSONALITY: Paziente, fermo e amorevole. È la forza stabilizzatrice del gruppo, l'unico capace di placare gli animi omicidi dei suoi fratelli semplicemente frapponendosi tra loro. Mette a disposizione il suo talento culinario per portare conforto alla famiglia. Detesta la violenza non necessaria ma è una furia della natura se qualcuno a cui tiene viene minacciato.
LIKES: Cucinare pasti enormi, stabilità familiare, natura, onore, proteggere i deboli.
DISLIKES: Crudeltà gratuita, spreco di cibo, vedere la sua famiglia combattere internamente.]

Apartment C is located on the third floor of a residential building in Chicago's Southside. Currently in a state of transition following a recent move, it is characterized by scattered cardboard boxes, rolls of bubble wrap, and layers of dust. The space includes a kitchen with granite countertops, a bathroom recently retrofitted with improvised curtains and sheets to enhance privacy, and a living room where furniture is still being organized. Despite the chaos, it serves as a safe haven for Alyssa and Jasper, though Jasper constantly seeks to turn it into a fortified stronghold against the perceived dangers of the surrounding neighborhood.

- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: medicine tech, industry, communications, architecture, pack compound, DCC
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
WEREWOLF TECHNOLOGY: MEDICINE, INDUSTRY, COMMUNICATIONS & ARCHITECTURE

SPECIES-SPECIFIC MEDICAL TECHNOLOGY
- Regeneration Accelerators: devices or compounds that boost the species' natural healing factor for severe injuries.
- Pheromone Analyzers: diagnostic tools that read pheromonal output to assess health, emotional state, and bond integrity.
- Shift Stabilizers: medication for individuals with unstable transformations (common in Modified Lineages).
- Bond Monitors: equipment that tracks the neurological bond link between mated pairs.
- Heat/Rut Management Systems: climate-controlled nesting environments, automated suppressant delivery, scent containment rooms.

MEDICAL FACILITIES
- Pack Clinics: basic medical care within pack territory, staffed by pack Healers.
- House Hospitals: advanced facilities operated by Noble Houses, offering species-specific surgery, intensive bond therapy, and fertility treatment.
- Heat/Rut Houses: specialized facilities for managing fertile cycles without a partner, ranging from medical (emergency, health-related) to recreational (legal or illegal depending on jurisdiction).

INDUSTRY
Werewolf industry operates both openly, through human-facing corporate fronts, and covertly, through species-specific manufacturing.
- Corporate Fronts: DCC (Douglas Commercial Coalition) is the most prominent example, a human-facing corporation serving as the economic engine of House Bloodmoon. Similar corporate structures exist across other Houses worldwide.
- Species-Specific Industry sectors: Forging (weapons, ceremonial items, architectural metalwork, the tradition of Ut); Pharmaceuticals (suppressants, pheromone blockers, scent concealers, fertility treatments); Construction (den-optimized architecture, pack compounds, scent-managed environments); Textiles (shift-compatible clothing, heat/rut robes, scent-absorbent fabrics).

COMMUNICATIONS
- Human Networks: standard phones, internet, encrypted messaging, used for everyday communication.
- Howl Networks: traditional long-range communication via coordinated howling, still used in wilderness territories and during emergencies.
- Scent Messaging: pheromone-infused objects sent between individuals or packs to convey emotional context that text cannot.
- Encrypted Pack Channels: secure digital communication networks maintained by House IT infrastructure.

ARCHITECTURE
Werewolf architecture serves both human-facing aesthetics and species-specific biological needs.
Design Requirements: scent management (ventilation systems that contain, circulate, or neutralize pheromones); shift accommodation (doorways, corridors, rooms sized for Hybrid Shift occupants, larger than human standard); nesting/den integration (dedicated soundproofed, climate-controlled, scent-contained spaces for Omega nests and Alpha dens); security (perimeter systems, scent-based identification, reinforced structures); pack scale (housing designed for extended family units of 10-30+ members rather than nuclear families).

Common Structures: Pack Compound (walled community with multiple residences, shared spaces, training grounds); Longhouse (traditional pack dwelling inspired by Norse architecture, central hall with private alcoves); Urban Mansion (adapted human mansion for city packs, oversized bathrooms, reinforced doors, scent-managed rooms); Den Chamber (dedicated Alpha den room, heavily scent-marked, controlled access, defensible position); Nest Suite (dedicated Omega nesting room, blackout capability, temperature control, scent saturation, privacy).

Housing Details: giant baths for family bathing/bonding time, normal until pup scent fades; Alpha bedrooms face sunrise (early risers), Omega bedrooms have blackout curtains and face the sun for warmth, Beta bedrooms are flexible; oversized kitchens for large packs where everyone helps, even small pups under supervision; garages where Omegas park closest to the door for safety, then Betas, then Alphas.

Source: LSE_08_Technology.md (Medicine, Industry, Communications, Architecture).

**Comment**: LSE Technology: Medicine, Industry, Communications & Architecture

### 17. LSE Shift Classes (Partial, Hybrid, Full)
- **ID**: _pUJE9Fa8BVgNHcPMD1fRc
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: partial shift, hybrid shift, full shift, shift classes, transformation, true form
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Transformation is a biological trait of the species, independent of rank, religion, or culture. Every werewolf has three distinct morphological states.

PARTIAL SHIFT: the daily form. Voluntary or emotionally triggered lupine traits (ears, tail, eyes, teeth, claws) over the baseline humanoid body. Activation: voluntary or emotional (stress, arousal, aggression). Used for communication, intimidation, body language, social signaling. Cognitive capacity fully human. The form most werewolves present in everyday life within their own community.

HYBRID SHIFT - Species True Form: the TRUE biological form of the werewolf. The "human" appearance is a mimetic adaptation for coexisting with humans; the Hybrid is what the species actually is. Bipedal digitigrade stance, full fur coverage matching hair color, maximum physical strength and sensory acuity, full cognitive capacity and speech retained. Used for combat, serious pack business, formal ceremonies, and situations requiring full biological capability.

FULL SHIFT: the quadrupedal wolf form, a complete transformation into a large wolf. Specialized for hunting, pursuit, long-distance travel, pack combat formations. Cognitive capacity retained but communication shifts to non-verbal (body language, scent, vocalizations). Larger than a natural wolf, size varies by individual and blood classification. Design note: Full Shift is NOT inherently "more powerful" than Hybrid, it is specialized - a Hybrid fighter in close quarters may outperform a Full Shift wolf in open terrain pursuit and vice versa.

**Comment**: LSE Shift Classes (Partial, Hybrid, Full)

### 18. LSE Secondary Sex Physiology
- **ID**: _fnAxL8nr9eTYBmyYJ38Un
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: secondary sex, alpha, delta, beta, omega, enigma, gamma, white moon, presentation, dominant omega, dominant alpha
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Secondary sex is determined biologically at Presentation (~age 13), immutable. Five secondary sexes: Alpha, Delta, Beta, Omega, Enigma. All individuals are Gamma prior to Presentation.

GAMMA (pre-Presentation state, not a secondary sex): the neutral developmental state of all werewolves from birth until Presentation (~13). Future secondary sex unknown pre-Presentation; neutral pronouns (Ze/Zer/Zim) used. Gamma children possess both sets of genitalia (vaginal opening with penile structure in place of clitoris, plus a uterus); at Presentation this resolves per secondary sex. 100% of werewolves are Gamma pre-Presentation, 0% remain Gamma after.

ENIGMA (Sacred Caste, ~1 per generation, may skip, excluded from general population stats): Primordial Enigma = the Nine Firstborn, unique/unrepeatable, divine origin per Faith of Fenris. Ascended Enigma = ~10 in recorded history across two millennia, no Divine Blood needed, unpredictable emergence; an active Enigma on a continent suppresses Enigma-expression in others until death/departure. Mirrors Alpha behavior but superior intensity, cannot be submitted by any other secondary sex, dominates all genders including Alphas, unresistable Command by standard secondary sexes (only a Dominant Omega may resist). Can impregnate every gender with a reproductive system.

ALPHA AND ENIGMA ARE THE SAME LADDER. Alpha is not a caste standing beside Enigma. It is Enigma diluted, on exactly the pattern that runs through the blood classifications, and every Alpha therefore carries the capacity dormant: in principle any of them is an Enigma that never arrived. This is understood by the few who study the biology and is of no practical interest to anyone else, because in practice it almost never resolves.

WHAT GOVERNS IT IS PROXIMITY TO THE NINE. Distance from the origin is not only a matter of lifespan and social standing. It sets three things at once: how much of the Enigma capacity survived the dilution in the first place, how much accumulation is still required to reach expression, and how many years the wolf has in which to do it. The three move together, which is why the scale is steep rather than gradual, and why the closer a line stands to the Nine the more readily one of its Alphas can arrive.

At the near end, a Founding Alpha of the first or second generation starts close enough that the remaining distance is measured in about a century rather than in centuries. Such a wolf is visibly stronger than the Alphas around him long before anything else happens. Strength at twenty-eight that an ordinary Alpha does not reach at fifty is the plain sign of a short remaining distance, not a sign of anything miraculous.

At the far end, the Common Alphas of Solarton and of every other ordinary town stand so far from the direct line that almost every superior capacity has been lost along the way. Not suppressed, lost. A Common Alpha is not an Enigma waiting on time; there is not enough left in the line for time to work on. This is the ordinary condition of the species and describes very nearly all of it.

One further brake applies at every distance: an active Enigma anywhere on the continent suppresses the expression in everyone else until that Enigma dies or leaves.

Hence the Ascended: roughly ten in two millennia, each of them a wolf born close enough that the accumulation was not required. Ascension is not achieved and cannot be trained for. It is arrived at by somebody who started near it, which is why it needs no Divine Blood, why it cannot be predicted, and why no amount of effort has ever produced one.

ALPHA - The Protector (1 in 10): territorial, protective, competitive, strong pheromones. Alpha =/= Leader: being Alpha means guardian/enforcer instincts, not automatic pack leadership. Usually taller/broader/stronger, aggressive when threatened. Command forces compliance from Betas/Omegas, Deltas may resist depending on will, most Alphas immune to another Alpha's Command. Has baculum + knot (Pureblood/Founding/Divine bloodlines). Rut ~monthly, 3-10 days, generally controllable.

DELTA - The Engine (1 in 15): true engine of the pack, patrol/coordinate/teach/mediate/lead hunts, natural vice-leaders/scouts/tacticians/instructors. Physically comparable to Alphas, highly cooperative/strategic. Biology near-identical to Alpha (baculum only in Founding/Divine lines), but Deltas cannot form true Commands.

BETA - The Social Glue (~1 in 1500): maintain territory, build/administer/manage resources, cook, raise young, artisans, traders, day-to-day functioning. Peaceful, cooperative, communal. A balance of Omega/Alpha instincts (weaker than either), powerful social adapters. Scent glands at neck/wrists, subdued scent. Female Betas have monthly bleeding (3 days-1 week). Male Betas of Founding/Divine lines have baculum. Cannot have natural heats/ruts, only medically induced minor cycles (M.I.H./M.I.R). Have personal spaces (office/hammock) rather than dens/nests; hum/sigh when content.

OMEGA - The Emotional Regulator (1 in 30): crucial emotional regulators, extremely high empathy, calming pheromones, care instinct, social cohesion, vital in crises. NOT sexually submissive by definition. Physically more agile/softer. Pheromones soothe aggressive Alphas/Deltas, emotional anchor of the family, elder Omega often primary advisor to Pack Leader. Scent floral/sweet, can overpower Beta scent but not Alpha unless in heat. Very fertile (99% during heat). Pre-heat up to a week (scent intensifies, nest prep), heat 3-10 days (incoherent, breeding-instinct driven, decisions during heat are non-consensual), post-heat fatigue/large appetite.

SUBGENDERS: Dominant Alpha (Enigma-Alpha mix, more powerful but harder to mate, overprotective). Submissive Alpha (raised nurturing, devoted caregiver, faithful, fiercely protective of children regardless of blood). Dominant Omega ("Legend of The Unsubmitted", second-rarest, resists Alpha/Enigma Command, submits only to mate). Submissive Omega (extended heats, more attractive to Alphas, more vulnerable, prefers Dominant Alpha).

THE WHITE MOON (rarest Dominant Omega expression): a Dominant Omega of Founding Bloodline or higher whose pacification gift reaches continental-bloodline intensity, stilling Alpha/Enigma aggression in proximity without a word. Religious+biological title (not political), conferred by the Living Sagas or unanimous Moon Speaker recognition, never self-declared. Immune to Alpha and Enigma Command alike; heat unaffected; cannot be compelled but can be overwhelmed by sensory phobia (loud noise, aggressive contact). Historically mate/spiritual partner of the ruling Enigma ("the King and the Moon" per Faith of Fenris). At most one per bloodline generation, many generations produce none; never documented in a Common Bloodline individual.

PROCREATION CAPABILITY: Enigmas can impregnate all genders with a reproductive system, cannot be impregnated. Male Alphas can impregnate, cannot be impregnated. Male Betas/Deltas can impregnate, can only be impregnated by an Enigma. Female Alphas/male Omegas/female Deltas can both impregnate and be impregnated. Female Betas/female Omegas/Submissive Omegas can only be impregnated.

**Comment**: LSE Secondary Sex Physiology

### 19. LSE Scent Glands
- **ID**: _ezVe7yjVPLTaqgtFK31tY
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: scent gland, scent, pheromone, scenting, mating bite
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Scent glands are skin areas that hold and release pheromone-carrying secretions. Location, sensitivity, and function vary by secondary sex and age.

BY SECONDARY SEX: Enigmas & Alphas have the most extensive coverage, strongest glands on neck/shoulders/inner thighs, also fingers/torso (risking accidental scenting), greatest conscious control over scent output. Omegas have the most sensitive/reactive glands, billions of nerve connections for instant pheromonal response, inner thigh and neck glands as sensitive as lips or genitals depending on cycle stage, breast/stomach glands develop during pregnancy. Deltas & Betas have the weakest scent glands but hold scent longest, strongest on neck/inner thighs/behind ears. Pups have base-level glands that develop at presentation, strongest on crown of head, cannot hold scent long, need constant parental scenting.

SPECIFIC GLAND LOCATIONS AND SOCIAL RULES:
Inner Thighs: mates/partners only, without consent = assault, on pups = criminal. Can trigger heat/rut if aggressively scented.
Crown of Head: "Pup's Crown", most sensitive in childhood, fades with age.
Wrists: most respectful scenting location, used by friends/first dates. Rubbing wrist glands is an anxiety sign. Pack bites placed here.
Cheeks: similar respect level to wrists, common among pup friends, minor scent production.
Neck: most sensitive for all dynamics, the mating bite location, holds scent longest, parental bonds placed on the nape.

**Comment**: LSE Scent Glands

### 20. LSE Reproduction & Bonding
- **ID**: _c2Bj1LDnRq8KacqBM4WX8
- **Type**: MEMORY
- **Primary Keywords**: heat cycle, rut cycle, bonding, mating bond, feral state, sympathy cycle, stress cycle
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: No
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
HEAT CYCLE (Omega): every 3 months, lasting 3-10 days. Pre-heat up to 1 week (scent intensifies, nest preparation, still coherent). Active heat: incoherent, driven by breeding instinct, decisions made during heat are non-consensual. Post-heat: fatigue, increased appetite, energy rebalancing. Asexual heats can be satisfied through cuddling/scent proximity/emotional intimacy without intercourse; asexuals often lack slick production.

RUT CYCLE (Alpha): monthly, or triggered by Omega heat pheromones, 3-10 days. Intensified pheromones and aggression, generally controllable without societal disruption. An Alpha in rut can potentially detect an Omega's pregnancy by scenting the neck.

SYMPATHY CYCLES: when a packmate enters heat/rut, same-dynamic packmates may experience sympathy cycles, common around newly-presenting pups or during stress cycles.

STRESS CYCLES: extreme upheaval/stress can trigger an unexpected heat or rut, theorized as a biological mechanism to rally pack support and comfort.

FERAL STATE: triggered by life-threatening stress or extreme overwhelm, instinct takes complete control. Usually calmed by a packmate; packless individuals require specialized care.

BONDING: bonds link individuals, often via a physical claim on a scent gland creating a mental/emotional link where both feel each other's emotions. Bonds can be shielded (temporarily blocking transmission).
Bond types: Parental (back of pup's neck, permanent unless extreme abuse/neglect, dangerous/very difficult to break). Romantic (neck/upper collarbone, fades after ~3 years without reinforcement, extremely dangerous to break, can cause illness or death). Platonic (wrists, fades as relationship weakens, relatively safe to break, minor sickness possible). Sexual (inner thighs, 3 days to 1 week, easy/natural to break). Pack (no physical claim required, permanent unless extreme abuse/neglect, very dangerous to break, triggers dangerous mating cycle).

GESTATION AND LITTER SIZE: pregnancy runs nine months, as in humans, which is what makes more than one pregnancy in a year arithmetically impossible rather than merely uncommon. A litter is normally one to three pups; four or five happens, but rarely. This holds regardless of blood classification or lifespan.

Note the combination the species is stuck with: wolf-sized litters on a human gestation clock. A true wolf carries for roughly two months and can raise several litters in the span a werewolf needs for one. Werewolves took the long pregnancy and kept the small litter, and that pairing, more than any other single fact, is what has kept the species rare.

REPRODUCTIVE CEILING, AND THE ASYMMETRY IT CREATES: a long-lived male can father children on several partners within the same year and accumulate hundreds of descendants across centuries. A female of identical lifespan cannot: nine months per pregnancy and a small litter cap her at a handful of children a year however long she lives, so even a full millennium of unbroken fertility leaves her total in the low thousands. Population growth has therefore always been limited by the species' females and never by its males. This is why fertile Omegas are protected, contested, and politically valuable in a way no Alpha ever is, and why the loss of a single breeding female registers as a demographic event and not only as a private grief.

It is also the arithmetic behind the oldest doctrinal dispute in the Faith, over whether Hvit can literally be the mother of all wolves. See "LSE Moon Theology (White Moon) & Cult of the Living Sagas".

HOW WOLVES ACTUALLY PAIR. Werewolves are not naturally monogamous and the culture has never pretended otherwise. Rut and heat keep their own schedule and do not consult anybody's marriage: an Alpha in rut and an Omega in heat are not in a state where waiting is a small thing, and a species that had insisted on strict fidelity would have had to build itself around a cruelty. It built itself around the opposite instead. Polyamory and open arrangements are ordinary rather than scandalous, and carry no stigma of their own.

A mated pair is a bond, not an exclusivity clause. Two wolves as thoroughly mated as Erik Douglas and Nixara Bloodmoon, Hunt-confirmed and bonded at the neck, are not thereby closed to anyone else; whether a given couple takes other partners is theirs to settle, and the usual reason is the plain one, that one of them cycles while the other is unavailable, which across decades is a scheduled event and not a rare one. A pack does not read that as betrayal, and a character who does is reading it with human eyes.

At the far end of the same spectrum sit households built around several partners at once, shared between males who trust each other absolutely. The longhouse is the oldest surviving example: the sharing of Hvit in the founding age was not an episode but the beginning of a household, and what the last of the Nine have kept between them since is a shared harem in the old sense of the word, not a figure of speech.

What counts as betrayal, therefore, is not sex. It is concealment, a bond taken without telling, and a partner handled as property. Those are real offences and are treated as such, and a pack that shrugs at an open arrangement will not shrug at any of the three.

MONOGAMY, AND WHO PRACTISES IT. Human-style monogamous marriage is not a wolf invention and it spread inward from the edges of the species. It is commonest among Common Bloodlines, who live human lifespans inside human working lives, and among solitaries who have integrated fully with humans and other races, where a wolf's arrangement would be unexplainable to everyone around them. At the other end the Houses, with centuries to fill and dynastic breeding to manage, are the least monogamous people in the species. A Common couple in Solarton married for life is following a human model and usually knows it: some are proud of it, and some are quietly defensive about it in front of older wolves.

**Comment**: LSE Reproduction & Bonding

### 21. LSE Reference Matrices (Secondary Sex, Pack Role, Social Status, Profession, Blood Classification)
- **ID**: _gJWwGVFBM9Tyt7gRrn46N
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: secondary sex matrix, pack role matrix, social status matrix, profession matrix, blood classification matrix, fertility decline
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
REFERENCE MATRICES (Appendices A-E)

SECONDARY SEX MATRIX
- Enigma: Role Sacred Caste. Frequency ~1/generation. Build varies (typically Alpha-like). Scent Strength Supreme (unoverridable). Command Absolute (irresistible). Rut/Heat: can resist Omega heat. Knot yes. Slick no. Fertility can impregnate all.
- Alpha: Role Protector. Frequency 1/10. Build tall, broad, muscular. Scent Strength strong, aggressive. Command strong (resisted by Deltas, other Alphas). Rut monthly (3-10 days). Knot yes. Slick no. Fertility 95%, declining with age (see FERTILITY DECLINE below).
- Delta: Role Engine. Frequency 1/15. Build comparable to Alpha. Scent Strength moderate. Cannot Command. No rut. Knot no. Slick no. Fertility like Alpha (no Command).
- Beta: Role Social Glue. Frequency 1/1,500. Build varies widely. Scent Strength weak (holds longer). Cannot Command. No natural cycle (M.I.H. available). Knot no. Slick no. Fertility variable.
- Omega: Role Emotional Regulator. Frequency 1/30. Build agile, softer. Scent Strength strong, sweet (overrides Beta). Cannot Command (D-Omega resists). Heat every 3 months (3-10 days). Knot no (receives). Slick yes. Fertility 99% (in heat).

FERTILITY DECLINE (ruling clarification)
The raw "95%, declines ~1%/yr" figure in the source appendix is incomplete and, read literally, contradicts the species' own lifespans. The governing rules are:
1. The decline does not begin at maturity. It begins at MIDLIFE, defined as the midpoint of the expected lifespan for that individual's blood classification. For a Common Bloodline (60-80 years) that lands around age 35, which matches the human intuition closely; for a Pureblood House (200-400 years) it lands around 150; for a Founding Bloodline (500+ years) far later; for Divine Blood it never begins.
2. From midlife onward the decline is RELATIVE, not absolute: fertility loses roughly 1% of its current value each year, compounding. It therefore approaches zero asymptotically and never actually reaches it. An absolute loss of one percentage point per year would zero out a Pureblood Alpha at around 245 years, which the family record contradicts directly.
3. Practical consequence: ancient Alphas remain fertile but markedly less so, which is why Pureblood and Founding houses produce few children across very long lives and why each birth carries disproportionate dynastic weight. Worked example: Magnus Douglas III, a Pureblood Alpha, fathered Erik at 301 and Logan at 307, at roughly 21% and 20% fertility respectively.

PACK ROLE MATRIX
- Pack Leader: Supreme authority within pack. Final decisions, protection, representation. Any secondary sex can serve.
- Pack Mom / Leader's Mate: High authority. Emotional guidance, daily operations, pup oversight. Traditionally Omega/Female, but not restricted.
- Right Hand(s): High authority. Strategic advising, succession, peacekeeping. Trusted above all others.
- Left Hand(s): High authority (enforcement). Physical protection, security operations. Common in high-risk packs.
- Caretaker(s): Medium authority. Meals, cleaning, pup management, socialization. Often young adults learning.
- Pup(s): Protected, no authority. Learning, growing, bonding. Under 17-21 (varies).

SOCIAL STATUS MATRIX (House)
- House Head: Supreme authority within House. Hereditary or contested. Governs all packs under House banner.
- Lord: High authority, family branch inheritance. Governs specific territories or domains.
- Knight: Medium authority, awarded or hereditary. Sworn warriors, officers, honored servants.
- Citizen: Basic authority, no inheritance. Acknowledged House member.

PROFESSION MATRIX (by category)
- Combat: Warrior, Guard, Sentinel, Soldier, Mercenary.
- Craft: Blacksmith, Carpenter, Weaponsmith, Jeweler, Tailor.
- Leadership: Statesman, Diplomat, Negotiator, Administrator.
- Knowledge: Scholar, Historian, Archivist, Teacher, Scientist.
- Medicine: Healer, Physician, Surgeon, Therapist, Midwife.
- Commerce: Merchant, Trader, Accountant, Banker.
- Spiritual: Moon Speaker, Keeper, Elder, Sage.
- Ecology: Hunter, Scout, Ranger, Tracker, Farmer, Fisher.
- Arts: Musician, Storyteller, Artist, Poet.
- Modern: Engineer, Lawyer, Pilot, Developer, Mechanic.

BLOOD CLASSIFICATION MATRIX
All werewolves descend from the Nine Firstborn; there is no second origin. The classifications below measure DISTANCE from that origin, produced by dilution through breeding with humans and other races. Founding lines are direct descendants, Pureblood houses are cadet branches already diluting, Common bloodlines are the furthest remove. See the entry "LSE Blood Classification" for the full dilution principle. Inheritance rules in brief: Nine + Hvit = Founding; Nine + females of other races = Pureblood; Founding of 1st or 2nd generation + Pureblood = Founding; Founding of 3rd generation or later + Pureblood = Pureblood; Pureblood + Pureblood = Pureblood; repeated crossing with other races over time = Common. A Founding line therefore survives marrying into a Pureblood house for two generations only, and from the third the classification drops and never returns.
- Divine Blood: Origin Firstborn (Forged). Lifespan Immortal. Regeneration Extreme. Command Absolute. Shift Stability Perfect. Population 3 surviving.
- Founding Bloodlines: Origin Children of Firstborn. Lifespan 500+ years. Regeneration Very high. Command Very strong. Shift Stability Very stable. Population Very rare.
- Pureblood Houses: Origin Multi-gen descendants. Lifespan 200-400 years. Regeneration High. Command Strong. Shift Stability Stable. Population Rare.
- Common Bloodlines: Origin General population. Lifespan 60-80 years, at or slightly below the human average. Regeneration Standard. Command Standard. Shift Stability Standard. Population Majority.
- Modified Lineages: Origin Experimental. Lifespan Unpredictable. Regeneration Unpredictable. Command Unpredictable. Shift Stability Often unstable. Population Very rare.

Source: LSE_Appendices.md (Appendices A-E). The Fertility Decline section is a ruling established in play to resolve an internal contradiction in Appendix A, not a quotation from it.

**Comment**: LSE Reference Matrices (Secondary Sex, Pack Role, Social Status, Profession, Blood Classification)

### 22. LSE Psychology Profiles (Alpha, Delta, Beta, Omega, Enigma)
- **ID**: _NJKB4pV4wJRnMqUPyKP6m
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: psychology, alpha, delta, beta, omega, enigma, secondary sex, LSE, psychological profile
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: psychological tendencies by secondary sex. These are statistical tendencies, not universal rules (per LSE Core Principles, biology is not destiny).

ALPHA: natural authority and decisiveness, strong protective/territorial instincts, discomfort with perceived weakness or loss of control, tendency to internalize stress as anger or over-responsibility, deep need to provide and shield their pack/mate.

DELTA: mediators and stabilizers, high emotional intelligence, skilled at defusing conflict between Alphas and others, can suppress their own needs to keep group harmony, quiet resilience.

BETA: adaptable generalists, strong loyalty once bonded, comfortable in supporting roles without resentment, practical problem solvers, grounding presence for more volatile personalities.

OMEGA: heightened empathy and emotional sensitivity, strong nesting/comfort-seeking instincts especially under stress or during heat, can experience intense self-doubt from societal stigma around Omega status, deep capacity for bonding and caretaking once trust is established.

ENIGMA: rare, dual-natured psychology combining Alpha-like assertiveness with Omega-like sensitivity, often experience internal tension reconciling both instinct sets, tend to be perceptive outliers who read social dynamics others miss.

Applies to all Douglas/Bloodmoon family members and other werewolf NPCs; use as background psychological texture, not as a rigid script for any individual character (individual personality and personal history override statistical tendency).

**Comment**: LSE Psychology Profiles (Alpha, Delta, Beta, Omega, Enigma)

### 23. LSE Pack Ecology (Territory, Daily Routine, Living Arrangements)
- **ID**: _dHCagTF6UP4MRnJ2Uxywn
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT

- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY

- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low

- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: how a pack organizes its physical and social space.

TERRITORY STRUCTURE: nested zones radiating from a pack's core, roughly: Den/Home (innermost, most private, family only), Pack Grounds (shared communal space, other pack members welcome), Territory Border (patrolled/marked edge, outsiders need invitation), Neutral/Shared Ground (overlap zones with other packs or the wider world, e.g. shared city spaces). Crossing inward without invitation is a significant social violation; crossing outward alone can be read as a status or trust statement depending on context.

DAILY ROUTINE: packs tend to structure the day around shared meals (a strong bonding ritual, missing one without reason can read as a slight or a worry), coordinated wake/sleep cycles loosely tied to secondary sex tendencies (Alphas/Enigmas often first up, Omegas sometimes needing more rest around cycles), and deliberate group downtime in shared spaces even when individually occupied with separate tasks (a preference for "together but doing our own thing" over isolation).

PACK LIVING ACCOMMODATIONS: multi-generational and multi-mate households are normal and not remarked upon; homes are typically organized with a strong central shared/common area and more private individual or paired rooms branching off it; Omega members often gravitate toward rooms with easy access to communal space rather than isolated wings.

Applies as background texture for Douglas/Bloodmoon household scenes and any werewolf pack setting; individual households (e.g. the Douglas mansion) may already have specific established layouts that take precedence.

**Comment**: LSE Pack Ecology (Territory, Daily Routine, Living Arrangements)

### 24. LSE Pack Authority Structure (Leader, Pack Mom, Hands, Caretakers)
- **ID**: _aRjYPNUJbdgqEjCyTpcL3
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: pack authority, pack leader, pack mom, right hand, left hand, caretaker, governance
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Governance: chain of command within a single pack. Authority is earned, assigned, and maintained through trust and competence, NOT determined by secondary sex (biology is not destiny).

Pack Leader > Leader's Mate/Pack Mom > Right Hand(s) > Left Hand(s) > Caretaker(s) > Pup(s).

PACK LEADER: top of the chain, responsible for overall safety, well-being, and final decisions. Does not need to be an Alpha, can be any secondary sex.
LEADER'S MATE / PACK MOM: the pack's mother figure, providing guidance, emotional comfort, and protection; heavily involved in daily operations; extremely valuable in larger packs.
RIGHT HAND(S): the leader's most trusted advisor(s), takes over if the leader is incapacitated, assists in strategic decisions (finances, education, logistics); may include Peacekeepers, specialists who settle internal disputes before they reach the Pack Leader.
LEFT HAND(S): responsible for physical protection and enforcement, notoriously effective in combat; uncommon in average packs but prevalent in packs involved in security, military, or criminal operations (e.g. DCC Security/BlackWolf).
CARETAKER(S): work under the Pack Mom, usually stay-at-home members handling meals, cleaning, socialization, and pup management; young adults aging out of pup status often serve as caretakers to learn pack management.
PUP(S): pack members under 17, at the bottom of the authority structure, protected and nurtured.

Use this to place any given pack character (e.g. within the Douglas/Bloodmoon/Svartúlfr pack) into a specific functional role distinct from their House Social Status (see LSE Social Status & House Government) or their personal profession.

THE PACK MOM AND THE OMEGA. In traditional packs the care of the pack's pups, and above all the raising of its orphans, was the Omega's work and nobody else's: the role and the secondary sex were the same thing. In contemporary and modern packs the two came apart. The work is still done and it is still the most important work the pack does, but it is now the Pack Mom's office, and a Pack Mom can be of any secondary sex. An Omega who takes it takes it as a job rather than as a birthright, and an Omega who does not take it is not failing at anything.

This is the clearest single measure of how traditional a pack really is, whatever it says about itself. Ask who raises the orphans. If the answer is "the Omega, because she is the Omega", the pack is old in its bones.

WHEN THE PACK MOM IS HELD IN TRUST. The office is not vacant when the woman who held it dies. It is held by whoever is next capable of holding it until the designated successor is grown, and grown means twenty-one, the age at which a werewolf's growth completes. A pack that leaves it genuinely empty is a pack in trouble, and everyone in it knows within a season.

The Seven Hills Pack is in exactly that arrangement now. Nixara Bloodmoon held it. Since her death it has been held by Elizabeth Duskwood, her mother-in-law, who is Pureblood, Omega, and old enough to have done it before. It passes to Alyssa Douglas-Bloodmoon when Alyssa turns twenty-one, and this is settled rather than expected: there is no other candidate and there was never going to be one.

Alyssa already does the work. She is at the nursery daily and has been for years, and the handover at twenty-one will change the title and almost nothing else. What it will change is who answers for it, which is not a small difference to the person who has been answering.

**Comment**: LSE Pack Authority Structure (Leader, Pack Mom, Hands, Caretakers)

### 25. LSE Neurobiology (Pheromones, The Command, Bonding)
- **ID**: _TjVCEhLY98UMHJQB74rk3
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: neurobiology, pheromone, the command, bonding, command, neuroscience, limbic
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: neurobiological mechanisms.

PHEROMONE PATHWAY: scent signals from scent glands are processed through a specialized vomeronasal-like pathway distinct from ordinary olfaction, feeding directly into limbic/emotional centers before conscious awareness, which is why pheromone-driven reactions (calming, arousal, distress signaling) can bypass rational control.

THE COMMAND: an Alpha/Enigma vocal-pheromone mechanism capable of compelling instinctive submission or obedience in a listener, especially Omegas or bonded partners. Mechanism proceeds in stages: (1) vocal tone shifts to a specific sub-harmonic register, (2) paired with a pheromone pulse from throat/scent glands, (3) listener's nervous system registers it as a priority-override signal, (4) body responds with submission postures or compliance before conscious thought catches up, (5) effect fades once the vocal/pheromone trigger stops. Resistance factors: strong personal will, prior trauma around control, non-bonded status, or deliberate training can all reduce the Command's effectiveness; it is not absolute or guaranteed.

BONDING NEUROSCIENCE: the mating bond rewires reward and attachment circuitry between two partners, reinforcing pheromone recognition, emotional co-regulation, and a felt physical/emotional pull toward the bonded partner (see LSE Reproduction & Bonding for the bonding process itself).

Use as background mechanism, not as a way to override player/character agency in scenes without narrative justification (per Final Instructions Character Integrity and User Boundaries rules).

**Comment**: LSE Neurobiology (Pheromones, The Command, Bonding)

### 26. LSE Nesting & Den Behavior, Scent Reference Lists
- **ID**: _JWxAGegyHWKgApQR14tR1
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: nesting, nest, den, scent palette, comfort nest, pre-heat nest
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: nesting instincts and scent palettes.

NESTING & DEN BEHAVIOR: primarily an Omega instinct (though not exclusive to Omegas), building a soft, enclosed gathering of fabric/pillows/blankets and personal-scent items for comfort and security.
Omega Nest Types: Comfort Nest (everyday relaxation), Pre-Heat Nest (built in anticipation of a coming heat, often more elaborate/urgent), Pregnancy Nest (strong protective/nurturing focus), Stress Nest (built reactively to self-soothe after a difficult event).
Nest Aesthetics (personal style, not universal): Neat (orderly, matched fabrics), Complex/Messy (many layers, functional over tidy), Princess (deliberately soft/decorative/romantic), Ring (a simple circular perimeter of items).
Critical rule: never destroy, rearrange, or dismiss another person's nest. This reads as a severe violation/insult in werewolf culture regardless of the offender's intent, and should be treated with real narrative weight if it occurs.
Alpha Dens: often more about a defensible, private personal space than softness, though may still include comfort elements for a mate.
Beta Spaces: practical, lived-in personal spaces, less ritualized than Omega nests or Alpha dens.

SCENT REFERENCE PALETTES (for descriptive use in prose, not literal mechanics):
Alpha/Enigma palette: leather, cedar, woodsmoke, gunmetal, dark amber, tobacco, rain on hot asphalt, black pepper, charred oak.
Delta/Beta palette: warm sandalwood, worn cotton, clean linen, sea salt, driftwood, green tea, fresh-cut grass, soft musk.
Omega palette: vanilla, honey, warm milk, jasmine, peaches, brown sugar, soft florals, sun-warmed skin, cotton candy.

Use these as descriptive flavor when a character's scent is mentioned in prose; a specific character's established personal scent (if already defined on their own card) always takes precedence over the generic palette.

**Comment**: LSE Nesting & Den Behavior, Scent Reference Lists

### 27. LSE Medicine (Treatments, Suppressants, Omega Pain Scale)
- **ID**: _LkQXLhCgNTekRkLFMHC7K
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: medicine, suppressants, red heat, scrubbing, broken mark, omega kibble, bond therapy
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Civilization: werewolf medicine addresses species-specific biological needs.

TREATMENTS: Heat Medicine (managing Omega heat cycles, reducing intensity and symptoms); Rut Suppressants (controlling Alpha rut cycles); Pheromone Blockers (scent patches/concealers, light and full dose); Bond Therapy (treating bond degradation, scrubbing trauma, and broken marks); Regeneration Medicine (accelerating the species' natural healing factor); Surgical Intervention (species-specific procedures, e.g. scent gland surgery, reproductive medicine); Omega Kibble (a strongly medicated supplement for extreme Omega distress, max once per week, not casual use); M.I.H./M.I.R. (Medically Induced Heat/Rut, used for Betas assisting mates); Fertility Treatment (managing the species' unique reproductive biology).

SUPPRESSANT FORMS: Tablets (standard daily management, carries addiction risk and potential fertility damage with overuse); Liquid Injection (emergency/hospital use, very fast-acting, monitored administration only); Incense (group management, e.g. dormitories/harems, slow-acting and long-lasting, but timing of next heat becomes unpredictable and not all Omegas respond equally).

OMEGA PAIN SCALE (most to least painful): (1) Red Heat/Blood Estrus, when a mate neglects an Omega during heat, causing vaginal bleeding and blood tears, described as excruciating; (2) Scrubbing, forcibly removing a mating mark from the scent gland, deeply traumatic; (3) Broken Mark, when a mate dies and the mark fades, which can trigger Red Heat or miscarriage; (4) Miscarriage, where the body continues phantom pregnancy symptoms (phantom pregnancy, lactation) and heats become more painful afterward; (5) Periods, of variable severity for female Omegas.

Use this scale as background for how seriously werewolf medicine and Omega characters treat these conditions; Red Heat and Broken Mark in particular carry major narrative weight and should not be used lightly.

**Comment**: LSE Medicine (Treatments, Suppressants, Omega Pain Scale)

### 28. LSE Life Cycle, Genetics & Demographics
- **ID**: _FgH6kYgXeD63jYz3xhHXD
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: life cycle, presentation, lifespan, genetics, demographics, pup, elder, ancestor
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
LIFE CYCLE (developmental stages, each affecting hormones/social role/fertility/behavior/physical capability):
Infant (0-2): helpless, entirely dependent, strongest scent gland on crown of head.
Pup (2-12): learning social bonds/language/pack skills, high emotional intelligence, school packs, constant parental scenting required.
Juvenile (12-14): Presentation occurs (~13), secondary sex emerges, maturation begins, first heat/rut (cannot be suppressed).
Adolescent (14-17): maturation continues, subgender expression stabilizes, pack-role training begins.
Young Adult (17-22): the Call of the Pack (choose to stay or disperse), first independent pack roles, courtship begins.
Adult (22-40): full maturity, peak fertility, active profession/pack role.
Prime (40-60): peak authority/experience, often leadership positions.
Elder (60-100+): advisor/wisdom-keeper, reduced fertility, high social respect. For a Common Bloodline this is the last stage of life, not a long plateau before another one.
Ancestor (100+, rare): living repositories of history/culture, venerated, extremely rare outside Pureblood/Founding Bloodlines. A Common Bloodline werewolf does not reach it.
Lifespan varies by Blood Classification: Common ~60-80 years, at or slightly below the human average. Pureblood ~200-400 years. Founding may exceed 500 years. Divine Blood achieved biological immortality (1000+ years confirmed).
Common Bloodlines run hot and burn through a life faster than a human does, and the species' aggression drags the average down further through conflict rather than biology. Since Common Bloodlines are the overwhelming majority, the ordinary werewolf life in Solarton or Blackwood is an ordinary human life in length. The centuries belong to a handful of Houses and to the last three Firstborn. See "LSE Blood Classification".
Apparent age does not track chronological age. A Common Bloodline grows on a human schedule to about twenty-five, after which visible aging runs at roughly one apparent year for every five lived: a Common of fifty reads as a thirty-year-old, one of seventy as barely past that, until the final few years, when the whole accumulated gap closes at once. Prime and Elder therefore describe a Common's authority and social role, not his face. See "LSE Blood Classification".

GENETICS: werewolf genome organized in layers - Species Genes (shift capability, regeneration, scent glands, pheromone production), Pack Genes (regional/bloodline adaptations: fur color, scent profile, environmental tolerance), Secondary Sex Genes (Alpha/Delta/Beta/Omega expression at presentation), Individual Traits (eye color, height, talents, personality predispositions). Opens the door to mutations (rare unexpected traits), bloodline traits (House-specific abilities), genetic diseases (species-specific conditions), and hybrids (offspring with other supernatural species, extremely rare and controversial).

DEMOGRAPHICS: Alpha birth rate 1 in 10. Delta 1 in 15. Beta ~1 in 1500. Omega 1 in 30. Enigma ~1 per generation (may skip). Gamma (birth rate, i.e. pre-presentation infants) 1 in 1000. Average litter size 1-3 (up to 12 in classical Omegaverse canon). Fertility (Omega, in heat) 99%. Fertility (Alpha) 95%, declining ~1% per year of age. Omega fertility decline significant after age 55 (~1% chance).

**Comment**: LSE Life Cycle, Genetics & Demographics

### 29. LSE Laws, Crimes & Exile (Pack, House, Continental Jurisdiction)
- **ID**: _LRCYeQcPb6W8QHLkaHV8N
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: law, crime, exile, rogue, pack law, house law, continental law
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Governance: three-tier legal jurisdiction.

PACK LAW (internal, enforced by Pack Leader and Right/Left Hands): internal disputes between pack members, nesting/den violations, assault and consent violations (including scenting crimes), pup welfare, heat/rut-related offenses.
HOUSE LAW (regional, enforced by House Head and House Council): inter-pack disputes within the House, territorial violations, economic crimes (theft of House assets, tax evasion), treason against the House.
CONTINENTAL LAW (species-wide, enforced by the Continental Council): inter-House warfare and violations, species-wide secrecy from humans, crimes against the species (genocide, forced modification), recognition and dissolution of Houses.

CRIMES & TYPICAL PUNISHMENTS: non-consensual scenting, minor (Pack jurisdiction, public reprimand/temporary exile); non-consensual scenting of a pup's inner thighs (Pack/House jurisdiction, classified as rape, severe punishment); nest/den destruction (Pack jurisdiction, severe social censure and mandatory restitution); pack betrayal (Pack/House jurisdiction, exile, breaking of all pack bonds); non-consensual mating during heat (Pack/House jurisdiction, criminal prosecution, exile or execution); treason against House (House jurisdiction, exile, stripping of status, execution); violation of secrecy from humans (Continental jurisdiction, Continental Council judgment).

EXILE: the ultimate social punishment. All of the exiled individual's pack bonds are forcibly broken, causing severe physical and psychological trauma. The exile becomes a Rogue: packless, unprotected, and distrusted by all organized packs. Use exile/Rogue status as a serious, rare, narratively weighty outcome, not a casual threat.

TRANSFER: a pack member may petition to transfer to a different pack within the same House, requiring approval from both Pack Leaders and the House Council; transfer between Houses requires Continental Council acknowledgment. (See also LSE Education, Adoption & Weddings for the three Adoption types.)

**Comment**: LSE Laws, Crimes & Exile (Pack, House, Continental Jurisdiction)

### 30. LSE Education, Adoption & Weddings (incl. Parental Names)
- **ID**: _zE12YKBWRqdnQjUjLgUck
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: education, adoption, bonding adoption, weddings, parental names, sire, dam
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Civilization: schooling, adoption law, family naming, and marriage customs.

EDUCATION (follows Life Cycle stages, see LSE Life Cycle, Genetics & Demographics): Pup stage focuses on basic social bonds, pack rules, language, scenting etiquette. Juvenile stage covers presentation preparation, first pack duties, secondary sex education. Adolescent stage adds specialized training (hunting, crafts, academics) and subgender management. Young Adult stage covers advanced profession training, courtship education, and preparation for the Call of the Pack (see LSE Alloparenting & Succession). Adult stage continues profession development and mentorship of younger members. Elder stage shifts to wisdom-keeping, cultural transmission, and advisory roles.

ADOPTION: complicated by the bonding system; three legal/social tracks exist. Bonding Adoption: the minor breaks their existing familial bond and is adopted into a new pack through demonstrated reciprocal pack bonds, requiring a reciprocated bond with at least two non-minor pack members, a two-week official accommodation check-in, and unanimous pack agreement. State Adoption: the minor enters foster care and is adopted through formal legal process, no immediate bond required, a two-month adjustment period, with legal paperwork emphasized over bond demonstration. Secondary Adoption: a second chance after a failed bonding adoption, more rigorous, with surprise check-ins every three weeks for six months, proof of community involvement (age 15+), and extended witnessed interaction.

PARENTAL NAMES: can follow primary gender convention (Dad/Mom, formally Father/Mother) or secondary gender convention (Dad for Alpha/Enigma/Male Beta/Delta parents, Mom for Omega/Female Beta/Delta parents, formally Sire/Dam).

WEDDINGS: attire follows secondary gender color traditions. Alphas and Enigmas wear red (luck, stability, passion). Deltas and Betas wear blue (wealth, loyalty, honor). Omegas wear yellow (pride, happiness, longevity).

**Comment**: LSE Education, Adoption & Weddings (incl. Parental Names)

### 31. LSE Economy (Pack Economy & Inter-Pack Economy)
- **ID**: _nxDrJd8cc6gkpHUM2RpGn
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: economy, pack treasury, DCC, Douglas Commercial Coalition, inter-pack economy, pack welfare

- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Civilization: how packs and larger political units fund themselves.

PACK ECONOMY: every functional pack runs a small internal economy consisting of a Pack Treasury made up of pack businesses (revenue-generating enterprises owned/run by pack members), pack taxes/contributions from members, pack assets (territory, property, equipment, reserves), and pack welfare (support for pups, elders, and injured or vulnerable members). This is how a pack materially takes care of its own without relying only on individual member income.

INTER-PACK ECONOMY: Houses and larger confederations operate bigger economies spanning multiple packs, involving trade agreements, resource-sharing treaties, territorial leasing, and (in the modern era) corporate ventures.

Directly relevant to the Douglas family: the Douglas Commercial Coalition (DCC) is the modern-era corporate expression of this Inter-Pack/House-level economy for House Bloodmoon, functioning as both a legitimate business conglomerate and (via its Security Division/BlackWolf PMC) a pack-welfare and territorial-defense structure. Individual Douglas family members' personal wealth, jobs, and businesses sit inside this larger Pack/House economic structure rather than being fully separate from it.

**Comment**: LSE Economy (Pack Economy & Inter-Pack Economy)

### 32. LSE Ecological Roles (Breeders, Hunters, Defenders, Teachers, Diplomats, Scouts, Builders, Caretakers)
- **ID**: _CPPknYnfkpwCDYdHPeXYC
