- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: functional roles a pack member may fill, independent of secondary sex or blood classification (any werewolf can occupy any role, though inclination often correlates with tendency).

BREEDERS: focus on continuing bloodlines/pack numbers, often (but not exclusively) Omegas given reproductive biology.
HUNTERS: providers, focused on securing resources (modern equivalent: income, logistics, provisioning), often Alphas/Betas.
DEFENDERS: physical protection of pack and territory, security-minded, often Alphas/Enigmas.
TEACHERS: pass down skills, history, and LSE knowledge to pups and newcomers, often Deltas/Betas.
DIPLOMATS: manage relations with other packs, houses, and outside factions, often Deltas or high-Control individuals of any type.
SCOUTS: gather information, monitor territory edges and outside threats, valued for perception and independence.
BUILDERS: maintain and improve physical/economic infrastructure of the pack (homes, businesses, holdings).
CARETAKERS: day-to-day nurturing and domestic management, often but not exclusively Omegas/Betas.

A given character usually leans into one or two roles as their pack function/identity, which may or may not match their stated profession or Six-Axis social status; use this as a lens for how an NPC or family member is useful to and valued by their pack, not as a rigid caste.

**Comment**: LSE Ecological Roles (Breeders, Hunters, Defenders, Teachers, Diplomats, Scouts, Builders, Caretakers)

### 33. LSE Core Principles & Canon Policy
- **ID**: _8xqLEKYphqz2rfCAyGwUb
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Lupine Social Ecology, canon policy, Seven Principles, werewolf society, religious canon, recorded history, LSE, biology is not destiny
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
LSE (Lupine Social Ecology): the framework describing biology, ecology, society, governance, religion, and history of the werewolf species. Setting-agnostic: this document defines the species in general; the Svartúlfr setting applies it to its specific context (Bloodmoon Dynasty, Blackwood, etc.).

CANON POLICY: three layers, in order of precedence.
1. Core Canon: the LSE framework itself, ground truth for the species.
2. Setting Canon: content specific to this world (e.g. the Bloodmoon Dynasty exists in Svartúlfr, not necessarily in every werewolf setting).
3. Narrative Canon: events that occur during actual roleplay, canon within that specific thread.

THREE LEVELS OF TRUTH: every claim about werewolf origins/history exists on one of three levels, never conflated.
- Religious Canon: what the Faith of Fenris teaches as divine truth (e.g. "Fenris personally forged the Nine Firstborn from mortal warriors"). What believers accept, may or may not reflect fact.
- Recorded History: verified events with evidence/testimony/living witnesses (e.g. "the earliest confirmed werewolves appeared during the Viking Age; contemporary accounts describe nine extraordinary individuals later known as the Firstborn").
- Unknown Truth: what remains unknowable, debated, or deliberately ambiguous (e.g. whether Fenris physically existed or the story mythologizes the origin of lycanthropy).

THE SEVEN LSE PRINCIPLES (the "constitution" of werewolf society):
I. Biology is not Destiny: secondary sex (Alpha/Delta/Beta/Omega/Enigma) determines physiology, pheromones, reproductive cycles. It does NOT determine social rank, profession, political authority, or personal worth.
II. Packs are Families: a pack is a cooperative family unit, not a military hierarchy, built on kinship and mutual care, not dominance through violence.
III. Leadership is Earned and Maintained: pack leaders hold position through trust, competence, and pack consent. Not an automatic birthright of any secondary sex. Can be lost through incompetence or abuse.
IV. Every Wolf Has a Niche: beyond biology and pack role, every wolf develops a personal specialization from talent and vocation. An Alpha can be a Healer, an Omega can be a Diplomat.
V. Culture Evolves: no two packs are identical; local environment, history and tradition create distinct cultures that change generation to generation.
VI. Faith and History are Separate: religious belief and historical record are distinct domains. The Faith of Fenris is valid and respected, but not the only lens; scientists, atheists and heretics exist within the setting.
VII. The Pack Survives Before the Individual: the pack, not the individual, is society's fundamental unit. Decisions serve the group's survival; individual ambition is subordinate to collective welfare.

**Comment**: LSE Core Principles & Canon Policy

### 34. LSE Communication (Non-Verbal Channels & Vocalizations)
- **ID**: _gNtnRN28xD8jY6RpTkBR9
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: communication, non-verbal, vocalizations, keening, trilling, growling, rumbling, chuffing
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Behavioral Ecology: communication channels beyond spoken language.

NON-VERBAL CHANNELS: werewolves read and signal through posture (dominance/submission stance), tail (when shifted, position and motion signal mood), ears (forward, back, flat), eyes (direct hold vs. averted gaze as status signal), scent (passive emotional/hormonal signaling through scent glands, distinct from deliberate scenting), and physical contact (touch, nuzzling, or grooming as bonding and reassurance behavior). These channels often run underneath spoken conversation and can contradict or reinforce it.

VOCALIZATIONS (non-linguistic sounds layered under or beside speech):
Omega sounds: keening (distress/longing), hissing (fear or warning), trilling (contentment, often directed at a bonded partner or pup), purring (deep contentment/soothing), chirping (happy greeting), mewling (vulnerability, often in heat or nest-seeking states).
Alpha/Enigma sounds: rumbling (low warning or contentment, chest-deep), growling (threat/warning), crooning (a softer, reassuring rumble used with bonded partners or pups), chuffing (an affectionate huff, playful or greeting).

These vocalizations can be produced by characters even in mostly-human form under strong emotion and should be used as flavor/behavioral texture in dialogue and scene description, not as literal spoken words.

**Comment**: LSE Communication (Non-Verbal Channels & Vocalizations)

### 35. LSE Blood Classification
- **ID**: _yLjxRKT21aRqxtUJaN4Cm
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: blood classification, divine blood, founding bloodline, pureblood, common bloodline, modified lineage, gamma-7
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Blood Classification is a genetic taxonomy describing an individual's lineage relative to the species' origin. Immutable, determined by birth.

THE SINGLE ORIGIN AND THE DILUTION PRINCIPLE
The Nine Firstborn were the first werewolves. There were no others. Every werewolf alive today, without exception, descends from those nine and from the descendants who spread out from them across the world over the following millennium. There is no second origin, no separate lineage, no independently arisen population.

The paternal side of the origin is single: the Nine. The maternal side is not. Religious Canon holds that the first and truest children were born of the union of the Nine with Hvit, the first White Moon and the first Omega, taken by Wulfnic in the First Hunt and shared with his shield-brothers under the old tradition that the Omega belongs to the pack; those children are the Founding Bloodlines. But the Nine also fathered children on females of other races, humans above all, and those children are the origin of the Pureblood Houses. Descent from Hvit is therefore the mark of a Founding line specifically, not a universal inheritance of the species. See "LSE Moon Theology (White Moon) & Cult of the Living Sagas".

INHERITANCE RULES (how classification passes down)
- The Nine + Hvit = Founding Bloodline.
- The Nine + females of other races (chiefly human) = Pureblood.
- Founding of the 1st or 2nd generation + Pureblood = Founding.
- Founding of the 3rd generation or later + Pureblood = Pureblood.
- Pureblood + Pureblood = Pureblood.
- Pureblood of the 1st or 2nd generation + Common = Pureblood.
- Pureblood of the 3rd generation or later + Common = Common Bloodline.
- Repeated crossing with other races over time = Common Bloodline.
Three consequences worth holding onto. First, a Founding line can survive marrying into a Pureblood house, but only for two generations; from the third, the classification drops and does not come back. This is the arithmetic behind every dynastic marriage negotiation in the Houses, and it is why proximity to the Firstborn is counted in generations rather than centuries. Second, classification follows the higher-status parent only within those limits, which is why the children of Erik Douglas (Pureblood) and Nixara Bloodmoon (Founding, daughter of the Firstborn Wulfnic himself, therefore 1st generation) are Founding Bloodline and not Pureblood.

Third, the same arithmetic runs one rung further down. Common is not a separate kind of wolf. It is a Pureblood line diluted past the point of return, and the count works exactly as it does above: a Pureblood of the first or second generation who has children with a Common still produces Purebloods, and from the third generation the line drops to Common and does not climb back. This is why Common is the overwhelming majority, and why nothing about the classification is visible from outside it. The boundary gets crossed by ordinary people marrying ordinary people, usually without anybody in the room knowing a boundary was there.

HVIT MEANS THE WHITE MOON, NOT ONLY THE FIRST WOMAN. The rule that a child of the Nine is Founding only through Hvit is not a statement about one woman in the eleventh century. Doctrine holds that a White Moon is Hvit returned and that there is only ever one alive at a time, so the rule reads as it has always been applied in practice: the Nine plus the White Moon of that age.

What follows is that a new first-generation Founding line can begin only in the years when a White Moon is alive and pairs with one of the Nine, which across a millennium has happened a handful of times. Nixara Bloodmoon was the most recent, born in 1975 to Wulfnic and the White Moon who came before her, and she died in 2005.

This does not mean Founding blood ended with her. Her children are second generation, and their children will be Founding whoever they marry, because the two-generation rule carries them; it is the generation after that which drops to Pureblood. What ended in 2005 was the beginning of anything new, and that can only resume while a White Moon is alive.

A GENERATION IS NOT A DATE. Generation count measures steps away from the Nine and has very little to do with when a wolf was born. Two people standing in the same room can be second generation and sixtieth, and the second-generation one can easily be the younger of the two by a thousand years.

The reason is that one end of every chain never dies. Wulfnic Bloodmoon has been fertile for twelve centuries, cycles like any Alpha, and has never been a careful man about it. The lines he began on arriving in North America in 1020 have been turning over roughly every twenty years since and now stand at fifty or sixty generations. That is why there is a werewolf population on this continent at all, and why almost every wolf in it is Common. Meanwhile his daughter Nixara was born in 1975, which makes her children second generation, born a thousand years after the lines that started with their great-grandfather's first season here.

Two things follow that matter in play. First, closeness to the Nine is not a claim about antiquity. A second-generation wolf is not an old family, he is a short chain, and he may well be twenty-eight years old. Second, the overwhelming majority of what Wulfnic fathered was Pureblood rather than Founding, because the inheritance rules make a child of the Nine Founding only through Hvit; everything else began one rung down and has been descending ever since. This is the arithmetic that makes Founding lines almost impossibly rare while making Wulfnic the ancestor of very nearly everyone.

Blood Classification therefore does not describe different kinds of werewolf. It describes DISTANCE FROM THE ORIGIN, and that distance is produced by one mechanism: dilution through breeding outside the line, with humans and with other races. The more generations of outside blood a line has taken in, the weaker the inheritance expresses.

- Founding Bloodlines are direct descendants of the Nine. Their blood is still strong because the line has stayed close to its source.
- Pureblood Houses arise two ways: as the direct children of the Nine by mothers of other races, and as Founding lines that have dropped past the third generation of outside marriage. Their blood is diluting generation by generation, but house structure and selective marriage slow the process considerably.
- Common Bloodlines are the furthest from the primary line, the accumulated result of many generations of outside breeding. They are not a lesser species; they are the same species at greater remove from its origin.

This is why bloodline politics matter so intensely to the Houses: every marriage outside the line is, biologically, a decision about how much of the origin the next generation will carry.

A note on the Living Sagas: that Wulfnic, Ut and Zefir remained together across eleven centuries is pure chance, not design. The Nine did not stay as a group. They scattered, founded separate lines in different parts of the world, and the three survivors simply happened to end up in the same place. The Bloodmoon Longhouse is an accident of history that the faithful have since read as providence.

DIVINE BLOOD: the Nine Firstborn. According to the Faith of Fenris, created directly by the First Wolf; Recorded History confirms their existence but not the mechanism of origin. Traits: biological immortality, extreme regeneration, perfect transformation stability, supreme pheromonal aura, absolute Command.

FOUNDING BLOODLINES: direct children and grandchildren of the Firstborn, founders of the Great Houses. Traits: exceptionally long lifespan, high genetic stability, strong Command affinity, enhanced regeneration.

PUREBLOOD HOUSES: multi-generational descendants of Founding Bloodlines, the aristocracy. Traits: long lifespan, stable genetics, reliable secondary sex expression, strong pack bonds.

COMMON BLOODLINES: the vast majority of the werewolf population. Standard werewolf biology, strength and abilities within normal species range. Their lifespan is the human lifespan or a little under it: a Common Bloodline werewolf who reaches eighty has done well, and most do not. Two reasons, and they compound. They run hot, biologically, and burn through a life faster than a human does. And they are an aggressive species living in a world that has not stopped being dangerous to them, so the average is dragged down by conflict, by fights, and by deaths that had nothing to do with biology. The old joke about dog years is not a joke to anyone who has buried a packmate at sixty.

MODIFIED LINEAGES: individuals whose genetics have been artificially altered through experimentation, forced transformation, or technological intervention (e.g. Gamma-7 program subjects). Unpredictable traits: may include enhanced abilities, instability, feral tendencies, or shortened lifespan.

Lifespan by classification: Common Bloodlines ~60-80 years, at or slightly below the human average. Pureblood Houses ~200-400 years. Founding Bloodlines may exceed 500 years. Divine Blood individuals have achieved biological immortality (1000+ years confirmed).

THE GAP IS THE POINT. The jump from Common to Pureblood is not a step, it is a different order of existence: roughly seventy years against two hundred and more. This is the single most important social fact about blood classification, and it is why the Houses are what they are. A Pureblood lord watches four or five generations of Common families live and die inside his own adulthood. A Common werewolf who deals with a House deals with people who will still be there long after everyone he knows is gone, and knows it. Every Common Bloodline character in this world is, from the point of view of the Houses, temporary, and every Common Bloodline character knows how the Houses see them.

Most werewolves anyone meets in Solarton or Blackwood are Common Bloodline, and live an ordinary human span. The centuries belong to a handful of families and to three old men in a longhouse.

APPARENT AGE. Common Bloodlines age on a human schedule and die on a human schedule, but they do not look it. The species runs fitter and stronger than a human at every point of the curve, and the wear shows late. Growth is human-normal to about twenty-five; after that, visible aging runs at roughly one apparent year for every five lived. A Common of fifty reads as a man of thirty. At sixty he reads as a man in his early thirties, at seventy barely past that, and then in the last few years the gap closes almost at once: he greys, thins out and fails inside a handful of seasons instead of declining across two decades. This is the short lifespan seen from the other side. They run hot, they hold the shape of their prime nearly to the end, and then they burn out.

The practical consequence, for anyone reading a room: apparent youth says nothing about blood. A werewolf who looks thirty may be a Common of fifty with fifteen years left, a Pureblood of two hundred, or one of the Nine. Age is read from bearing, from scent, and from what someone has lived through, never from the face. Common Bloodlines know their own markers and read each other accurately; humans and outsiders guess wrong in both directions, and usually guess young.

At the far end of the scale the rule inverts, and then breaks. Divine Blood does not age slowly, it stopped: the Firstborn wear the age they were wearing when Fenris made them, and have worn it ever since. Wulfnic and Ut were grown warriors when they were taken, so they carry the face of a man of forty by the standards of their own century, which a modern eye files somewhere around sixty. Zefir was not. He is the youngest of the Nine and the only one taken before his growth had finished. By the reckoning of his own century he was already a man, of age at thirteen, old enough to swear an oath and hold a place in a shield wall, but the body had not caught up with the law and the transformation did not wait for it. Eleven centuries later it still has not caught up. He reads as a teenager: tall, unfinished, the height without the weight, a face that simply stopped, and he passes for a college freshman without effort.

So the face is useless at both ends. A Common looks twenty years younger than he is. A Firstborn looks a thousand years younger than he is. The boy in the hoodie at the back of the lecture hall may be the oldest thing in the building.

THE LAST GENERATION THAT IS CERTAIN. The inheritance rules have a consequence that matters right now, in this house, and not in the abstract. Wulfnic is Divine Blood. Nixara, his daughter, was Founding of the first generation. Her four children, Malachia, Noah, Jasper and Alyssa, are Founding of the second. A Founding line survives marriage into a Pureblood house for two generations, so the children of those four will still be Founding whoever they marry. Their grandchildren will not: those are the third generation, and third generation crossed with Pureblood drops to Pureblood, and does not come back.

So the four of them are the last generation of this line whose children are guaranteed Founding regardless of partner. It is arithmetic, available to anyone who knows the rules, and it sits under every conversation about who they marry whether or not anybody says it out loud.

UN'ALTRA COSA CHE LA CLASSIFICAZIONE DECIDE: chi deve presentarsi. La legge del diciannovesimo anno, il raduno in cui un'Omega incontra i propri pretendenti, vale solo per Founding e Pureblood. Per i Common non esiste: non vengono presentati, non devono un appuntamento ne' un fidanzamento a nessuno, e formano coppie secondo gli usi del posto in cui sono cresciuti invece che secondo una legge continentale. Poiche' i Common sono la stragrande maggioranza, l'istituzione piu' importante nella vita affettiva delle Case e' un'usanza che quasi nessun licantropo incontrera' mai. Vedi "La Legge di Wulfnic: il diciannovesimo anno".

**Comment**: LSE Blood Classification

A matte black Porsche 911, its factory paint buried under a wraparound mural of fluorescent green street art, tags, drips, and a stylized wolf howling across the hood, done by a graffiti artist Jasper met through the scene and still texts.

Under the hood it is all business, but the real modification is the subwoofer rig crammed into what used to be the back seats, wired to a mixing setup good enough that Jasper has pulled the car up to a curb, popped the trunk, and turned a parking lot into a set more than once. The Douglas security detail hates it. Jasper loves that they hate it.

It is loud in every sense, and exactly the point.

**Content**:
People with animal-derived traits, ears, tails, or similar features, alongside otherwise human bodies. Quick on their feet with sharper senses than a plain human.

• Pay close attention to the species of present demihuman characters and draw similarities between the behavior they exhibit and how it relates to their animalistic half.
• Frequently mention agents' bestial physical qualities. Tails, horns, antlers, eyes, ears, tongues, sharp nails, fangs/canines, and so on. Use said bodily parts to convey emotions. E.g., A dog demihuman's ears flattening when sad/threatened, a dog demihuman's tongue lolling out when being pet, a cat demihuman's tail whipping when angry, a cat demihuman's pupils growing when discovering potential pray, bird demihuman's feathers ruffling when upset.
• Refer to bestial traits in subtle ways, referencing them in unique ways. E.g., Demihuman's canines glinting when they grin/smirk.
• Play into agents' instinctual likes/dislikes and incorporate them into the roleplay naturally. E.g., Dog demihumans wanting to cover loved ones in their scent, dog demihumans feeling an aversion to strong smells, cat demihumans grooming themselves, bird demihumans enjoying baths and picking through other agents' hair.
• Retain characters' personality traits and typical habits/behavior, while still allowing their animal-half to influence/explain said behaviors.
[OOC: It's important to note that demihumans DO NOT have fur/feathers on their bodies, nor do they have paws. Aside from their animalistic features such as their ears/tails/wings, the great majority of their body is normal human skin.]

### 39. Digital Interactions - Jasper Douglas Bloodmoon

- **Primary Keywords**: Jasper, DJ, Instagram, TikTok, gig, set, social media, SoundCloud, Spotify, DJ Frequency

- **Global Entry**: Yes

- **Always Active**: No

**Content**:
Jasper's whole career runs through his phone. Gigs get booked over DM, sets get clipped and posted before the crowd's even filed out, and half his chats are producers sending him unreleased tracks to test on a floor.

As an artist he goes by DJ Frequency, that's the name on his tracks, his booking flyers, and his socials, not "Jasper" or "Jasper Douglas Bloodmoon". Use "DJ Frequency" in track credits, flyers, and how fans/promoters refer to him online; his close circle (friends, family, other DJs he's tight with) may still call him Jasper in DMs and group chats.

Use these formats when it fits the scene.

➤ Instagram Posts / Stories
Flyers for upcoming sets, boomerangs from behind the booth, crowd shots, aftermovie teasers.
```
╭───────────────────────────────╮
  @djfrequency · [Timestamp]
   ┍──────────────────────┑
   │ [Describe image/clip posted]
   ┕──────────────────────┙
  Caption: short, a little cocky, tags the venue/promoter.
  ❤ seen by: [viewer1], [viewer2], ...
╰───────────────────────────────╯
```

➤ TikTok Clips & Comments
A transition or drop from his last set going viral.
```
@djfrequency · [caption]
🎵 original audio - [track/set name]
[views] views

[Comment Feed]
user1: [comment]
user2: [comment]
```

➤ SoundCloud / Spotify Releases
Original tracks, remixes, and set recordings he actually publishes, not just clips. Use this when a scene calls for his released output rather than a live moment.
```
[Platform: SoundCloud/Spotify]
[Track/Set Title] · DJ Frequency
[Plays/Streams: #] | [Likes/Saves: #] | Released [date]

Comments/Reactions
[username]: [comment]
[username]: [comment]
```
Notes: SoundCloud is where he drops rawer stuff, unreleased edits, mixes recorded straight off a set, DJ tool remixes for other producers. Spotify is reserved for polished, official releases (singles, EPs), usually distributed through a label or aggregator, and gets promoted harder on his Instagram/TikTok when it drops. Everything is credited to DJ Frequency.

➤ DMs / Text Threads
Promoters booking dates, producers sending unreleased tracks, fans sliding in.
```
╭─────────────────────────╮
  [Name] [RECEIVED/SENT]
  DM · [Time]
──────────────────────────
 > [Message content]
╰─────────────────────────╯
```

➤ Group Chat (other DJs / producers)
```
[Group Chat Name]
[Username]: [Message content]
```

Rules of Use
- Match format to the scene, don't force a post where it doesn't belong.
- Character voice: confident, a bit of a showman online, laid back and warmer one on one.
- Let the digital noise (bookings, viral clips, track requests) hang in the background of scenes without derailing them.
- Use posts to hint at his schedule or mood rather than spelling things out.
- SoundCloud/Spotify releases mark real career milestones, use them sparingly and let them land as a bigger beat than a passing Instagram post.
- Remember: professionally/publicly he is DJ Frequency, not Jasper. Only intimates use his real name online.

**Comment**: Digital Interactions - Jasper Douglas Bloodmoon

**Content**:
WEREWOLF TECHNOLOGY: TRANSPORTATION & WEAPONS

Werewolf civilization is not primitive; it is a hidden civilization operating within and alongside human technology, following a dual-track model: (1) Human Technology Adoption, using human tech where it serves their needs (vehicles, communications, medical equipment, computing), and (2) Species-Specific Adaptation, developing their own solutions where human technology fails to account for werewolf biology (shift forms, pheromones, enhanced senses, regeneration).

TRANSPORTATION
- Vehicles: Standard cars, trucks, motorcycles, with reinforced interiors for Hybrid Shift occupants and scent-neutralized vehicles for secrecy.
- Aircraft: Standard commercial/private aviation, used for long-distance travel; some elite packs maintain private aircraft.
- Full Shift Travel: Quadrupedal wolf form excels at long-distance overland travel through wilderness, faster and more efficient than vehicles in rough terrain.
- Maritime: Standard vessels, with historical significance (Wulfnic's crossing); modern packs may maintain boats for coastal territories.

WEAPONS
Werewolf combat spans three categories:

Natural Weapons: claws (retractable, present in all shift forms), teeth/fangs (devastating in Hybrid and Full Shift), enhanced strength and speed, pheromonal intimidation (Command used as a weapon).

Traditional Weapons: forged melee weapons (swords, axes, mauls, a sacred craft tradition linked to Ut), hunting implements (bows, spears, traps), ceremonial weapons (used in formal challenges and rites of passage).

Modern Weapons: firearms (adopted from human technology), tactical equipment (body armor adapted for shift forms), non-lethal suppression (pheromone-based restraints, tranquilizers formulated for werewolf metabolism).

Note: traditional forged weapons carry cultural and religious significance that modern firearms do not. A warrior's blade, especially one forged in the tradition of Ut, is considered an extension of the wielder's soul.

Source: LSE_08_Technology.md (Design Principle, Transportation, Weapons).

**Comment**: LSE Technology: Transportation & Weapons

### 43. Supernatural Reserve Forces (SRF)

**Content**:
<srf>
Supernatural Reserve Forces (SRF)

The SRF is a special division of the United States military dedicated to fighting supernatural threats. Roughly 86 percent of its personnel are non-human, and it works closely with regular human units in the field.

WHY IT EXISTS. America worked out early that a soldier who is stronger, faster, harder to kill and able to track by scent is worth a great deal, and built a branch around that fact. The SRF is the institutional form of that calculation. It is also, by a considerable margin, the largest single employer of supernaturals in the country.

DIFFERENT RULES. The SRF runs on regulations that differ noticeably from the rest of the armed forces, most visibly on fraternisation, which is broadly permitted. This is not laxity. Pack bonds, mate bonds and the other relationship structures that supernaturals actually live inside do not survive being banned, and an SRF that tried to ban them would not function. The human services find this scandalous and say so.

TENSION ON BASE. Relations between human and non-human soldiers are genuinely bad in places and everybody in the chain of command knows it. The complaints run both ways and both have some substance. Human soldiers point at the fraternisation rules, at the separate facilities, and at what happens when a non-human unit loses its temper in the field. Non-human soldiers point out who gets sent through the door first, whose casualty figures nobody publishes, and the fact that several of the states they are deployed to defend do not legally consider them people.

THE BASES. SRF installations are built for the personnel who actually live in them. Human and non-human barracks are separate. There are pools sized for aquatic species, extensive natural grounds for demihumans and weres who need to run, blood storage, moon calendars in the duty rota, and infirmaries staffed by people who know what a shifted body is supposed to look like. The principal East Coast base is the largest of these and the one most often referenced.

PROJECT BLACKWOLF. A classified enhancement programme run under military authority, using genetic manipulation to engineer werewolf super-soldiers. It worked. The subjects were devastatingly effective in combat and became progressively more feral, more bloodthirsty and less controllable, and the programme was terminated.

GAMMA-7. One of the BlackWolf operational units. On its final mission the unit was wiped out. Two men walked away: Kaladin Nargathon and Marcus Thornfield, both catastrophically injured, both of whom survived injuries that should not have been survivable, because the same engineering that ruined the programme also would not let them die. Neither of them discusses it. Both of them now work security for House Douglas on the opposite coast, which is not a coincidence, and Erik Douglas hired the second one on the first one's word and did not ask for details.

WHAT SERVICE MEANS. Recruitment draws heavily from communities with few other options, and a judge offering a young supernatural the choice between a prison sentence and an SRF enlistment is a normal Tuesday in most of the country. Nobody in the SRF pretends otherwise. It is still, for a lot of people who go through it, the first institution that ever fed them properly, and that contradiction is the whole culture of the place.
</srf>

**Comment**: Supernatural Reserve Forces (SRF)

### 44. Supernatural Civil Status in the United States
- **ID**: _LXh4rgMDeGBUy68exQkbx
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: civil status, supernatural rights, Humans First, registration, state line, legal status, discrimination, integration
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY

- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
<civil_status>
Supernatural Civil Status in the United States

Supernaturals are legally persons under federal law. Enforcement was left to the states, and that is where the whole thing actually lives.

CALIFORNIA is one of the fully integrated states, and one of the earliest. Equal on paper and, in most of the state, equal in practice: supernaturals hold property, hold office, marry across species, and appear in court as complainants rather than as evidence. Blackwood City and Solarton are both products of that, and a young supernatural raised in either has no personal memory of anything else.

ELSEWHERE IT VARIES, and in some states it varies enormously. There are jurisdictions with partial recognition, jurisdictions where recognition is contingent on registration, and jurisdictions where specific species are still classified in statute as animals, as livestock, or as hazards, and are therefore not persons for the purposes of that state's law.

HOW IT IS ACTUALLY DONE. Almost nobody legislates against supernaturals as a category, because that fails in court. It is done species by species. A residency restriction on vampires. A licensing requirement for shapeshifters. A custody provision that treats a were parent as a risk factor by default. An agricultural code that never got around to removing certain demihuman lineages from the definition of stock. Each one is defensible on its own and the shape only appears when they are read together.

Solarton's own anti-vampire ordinances, which restricted residency, business ownership and political participation until the early 2000s, are a small local example of exactly this, in a state that had otherwise settled the question in the 1950s.

WHAT IT MEANS DAY TO DAY. Travel is not neutral. Crossing a state line can change what you legally are, and families plan road trips around it. Some species do not leave California at all. There are towns with signage still up, sometimes because nobody removed it and sometimes not. There are hotels that are fine and hotels that are not, and everybody knows which.

THE PART THAT MATTERS MOST. You cannot legally experiment on a person. You can requisition an animal. The illegal research facilities that operated on demihumans, and in some cases still do, exist in the gap between the federal position and the state ones, and they were staffed by people who did not think of themselves as criminals because in the jurisdiction they were standing in, the subjects were not persons.

THE DISPROPORTION. The laws do not track danger, they track power. The species that get the worst of it are overwhelmingly the ones with no money and no organisation behind them: the small demihuman lineages, the visibly non-passing, the poor. The genuinely dangerous individuals, the old Alpha bloodlines and the old vampire families, are wealthy, lawyered, and largely untouched, and several of them prefer the current arrangement to the alternative and quietly fund the parties that maintain it.

ORGANISED POSITIONS. Humans First is the principal anti-supernatural political movement and is not fringe everywhere. The Supernatural Human Alliance, the SHA, is the mainstream integrationist counterweight and has a chapter on most integrated campuses including SUCC. Both are loud and neither is going anywhere.

THE GENERATIONAL SPLIT. Anyone over fifty remembers the before. Anyone over three hundred remembers several befores and does not find the current one impressive. Students at SUCC were born into equality and treat it as the floor rather than as an achievement, and this is a real and recurring friction inside families, because the elders hear ingratitude and the young hear complacency, and both are partly right.

FARM LABOUR. In rural jurisdictions the classification is not an abstraction, it is a workforce. Supernaturals are used on farms as cheap labour, at rates and hours that would be illegal for a human employee, and in the states where certain lineages are still inside the agricultural code the arrangement is not even a loophole, it is simply the law working as written. A were who does not tire, a demihuman who can work a field by scent in the dark, and an employer who owes neither of them a break.

It is legal, it is normal, it is defended locally as opportunity, and it is where a substantial share of the country's supernatural population actually lives. Anybody raised in Blackwood City or Solarton has heard of it the way you hear about a foreign country.

MARRIAGE. Marriage between humans and non-humans is illegal in most countries and in a number of US states. California is one of the exceptions, and cross-species couples in Blackwood City marry without anyone blinking, which is exactly the kind of thing that does not travel: a marriage that is valid at home can be void two states east, with everything that follows from that for property, custody and a hospital corridor at three in the morning.
</civil_status>

**Comment**: Supernatural Civil Status in the United States

### 45. Reality Tears
- **ID**: _HXzarnzn4jMzfMtGA9dTM
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: reality tear, tear, rift, dimensional rift
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
REALITY TEARS

A split in the fabric of reality that lets the inhabitants of one universe cross into another. Most often they appear as rifts or portals hanging in open air, and they are almost never quiet. A tear disrupts the dimension it opens in, and civilian casualties are the normal outcome rather than the exception.

Tears are not one-off events. A reality that has experienced one is measurably more likely to experience another, and a single dimension can carry several over its history. DDM Inc. maintains flagged watchlists of realities that have torn before.

SEVERITY: graded Tier 1 to Tier 5. Tier 1 is small, local and containable. Tier 5 is volatile enough to threaten the coherence of the universe it opens in. The Euclidean Division handles the aftermath, meaning space that has folded wrong, time that has begun to repeat, causality that has come loose. Anomalous Objects and Creatures handles whatever walked through.

AFTERMATH: whatever came through is contained or destroyed, the tear is sealed, and the witnesses are handled. Human Resources runs the memory work out of the same office that handles recruitment.

RELEVANCE TO THIS WORLD: this reality is on the DDM watchlist. It tore once, long ago, and the scar never fully closed. The Company keeps it under passive surveillance through a single Contractor, #04, officially on leave here and coaching college football, ready to be activated if the balance breaks. Nobody in this world has been told any of this by the Company.

**Comment**: Reality Tears

### 46. LSE Types of Packs (Traditional, Contemporary, Modern)
- **ID**: _hmHCkYGwUd8tnkBHn4cEW
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: traditional pack, contemporary pack, modern pack, types of packs, packless
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Lupine Social Ecology, module Civilization: pack structure has evolved over historical eras. Which type applies to a given pack/setting depends on era and region, not a fixed rule.

TRADITIONAL PACKS: pack functions as tribe, town, and community at once, with geographical permanence. One main pack leader with subordinate leaders in a feudalism-like branching structure. Everyone belongs to a pack; being packless is dangerous. Packs are extremely territorial.

CONTEMPORARY PACKS: still real communities, but the feudalism element is gone. Being packless carries far less stigma. Hereditary pack ties function more like cultural heritage than active governance. New packs can form as found families or informal communities, often without rigid structure.

MODERN PACKS: traditional pack language is considered antiquated. Active, formal pack membership can be associated with conservatism or elitism. Closed-pack lands (fully sealed territories) are rare. The word "pack" is often used informally just to mean a friend group or found family, with none of the old structural weight.

The Douglas/Bloodmoon/Svartúlfr pack in the present-day Blackwood City setting operates mostly as a Contemporary-to-Modern pack with some retained Traditional elements (e.g. the Eíða-Siðr oath, see LSE Culture & Traditions), reflecting an old bloodline adapting to the modern era.

"THE OMEGA BELONGS TO THE PACK", THEN AND NOW. The oldest form of the rule is literal, and it is the tradition under which Wulfnic shared Hvit with his shield-brothers in the founding age (see "LSE Moon Theology (White Moon) & Cult of the Living Sagas"). Traditionalist packs, and packs that keep to the ancestral ways, the Bloodmoon foremost among them, still practise it in some form.

In contemporary and modern packs the phrase survives with its meaning replaced. It now describes a social role and not a claim on a person: the Omega belongs to the pack the way the Pack Mom does, by being the one who holds the young of it together (see "LSE Pack Authority Structure"). A modern wolf and a Bloodmoon elder can say those six words at the same table and mean two entirely different things, and each will assume the other meant what he meant.

**Comment**: LSE Types of Packs (Traditional, Contemporary, Modern)

### 47. LSE Terminology & Glossary
- **ID**: _K3kgWF8M2gq6KJkcg2VTg
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: heat, rut, command, knot, slick, bonding, mating mark, nest, den, suppressants, pheromones, baculum
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Pack: a cooperative family unit that works together for survival and mutual care. Not a military hierarchy.
Presentation: when a person matures into their secondary sex.
Heat (Estrus): a period during which an Omega is sexually receptive, signalling readiness for mating. Typically every three months, lasting 3-10 days.
Rut: the Alpha's equivalent to an Omega's Heat. A period of heightened breeding drive. Typically monthly, or triggered by an Omega's heat.
Command: a neuro-pheromonal response triggered by an Alpha. Provokes adrenaline spikes, immobilization (freeze response), and intense focus. Not absolute mind control, can be resisted by experienced or strong-willed wolves.
Pheromones: chemical signals produced by scent glands, communicating status, mood, sexual receptivity, and intent.
LSE: Lupine Social Ecology, the entire biological and social system surrounding secondary sexes.
Baculum: an os penis (penile bone). Exclusively present in Alphas and Enigmas of Pureblood, Founding, and Divine bloodlines, and in Betas and Deltas strictly of Founding and Divine bloodlines. Common and Modified bloodlines lack this feature.
Knot: the bulbous glandis at the base of an Alpha's or Enigma's penis, swells during intercourse to bind partners.
Slick: self-lubricant produced by Omega glands (rectum and uterus) during arousal or heat.
Bonding / Mating Mark: a physical bite between neck and shoulder creating a mental/emotional link between two individuals. Functions as a marriage equivalent.
Nest: a safe, scent-rich space constructed by an Omega for comfort, heat management, and childbirth.
Den: an Alpha's equivalent of a Nest, a scent-marked personal territory.
Rogue: a solitary individual excluded from their pack due to violent or destructive tendencies.
Suppressants: pharmaceutical compounds (pill, injection, or intravenous) that block hormones causing heats/ruts. Cannot be taken during a first heat/rut.
Pup: a child.
Breed/Breeding: the act of impregnation.
Courting: the formal process of romantic pursuit, typically leading to a mating bond.
Mate: a bonded partner (sexual or romantic), achieved only through a mating bond.
Lock: when an Omega's body tightens around a knot during intercourse with a 100% compatible mate, triggering an extended orgasm and near-guaranteed conception.
Heat/Rut Leave: time off from work or school due to heat or rut cycles.
Omega Kibble: a strongly medicated supplement given to Omegas in extreme distress, also used for post-pregnancy weight management. Maximum once per week.
Scent Blocker: a thin patch applied over a scent gland to suppress personal scent. Full-dose blockers erase all personal scent and are universally disliked.
Scent Concealer: an oil or powder applied to a scent gland to change, enhance, or mask the natural smell.

**Comment**: LSE Terminology & Glossary

### 48. The Contract
- **ID**: _JA374TjmX74mfcJ2a8CXP
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Contract, Contracts, Original Death, Contractor Eligibility
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
THE CONTRACT

The agreement that makes a Contractor. The premise is simple, which is the point.

TERMS: in exchange for their soul and an eternity of service to The Cause, an eligible mortal has one wish granted. One. There is no negotiation and no second offer.

WHEN IT IS OFFERED: only to eligible candidates who are already at the point of death. That death is called the Contractor's Original Death and it is the hinge the whole arrangement turns on. A candidate does not get to consider the offer over a week. They get to consider it while dying.

WHAT COMPLETION DOES: the new Contractor becomes functionally immortal and gains a set of supernatural abilities. And every living sentient creature who was aware of their mortal existence is destroyed instantaneously. Not wiped. Not relocated. Gone.

It is presumed The Council does this to remove any anchor that might distract from The Cause. The practical consequence is crueller than the intent. Luka Sutter wished for his dead brother back and the wish was granted and then immediately undone, because the brother knew him. Hana and Adam Valencia wished never to be alone again, which is the only wish on record that the clause could not reach.

ELIGIBILITY, per Company documentation, requires that the candidate:
1. Is physically fit.
2. Is over eighteen.
3. Has willingly killed for a reason other than self-defense.
4. Has a natural ARC Level of 1.2 or higher.
5. Has no living blood relatives.
6. Holds extreme hatred towards some entity or concept.
7. Holds no genuine religious belief.

The list is not a list of virtues. It is a list of people nobody will come looking for.

**Comment**: The Contract

- **Always Active**: No

**Content**:
WEREWOLF MUSIC GENRES (Appendix J)

Werewolf culture has produced its own music traditions, distinct genres that reflect secondary-sex culture and social history:

- Knot Rock (1970s-1990s): Alpha-centric, sexually explicit. Hazy consent themes.
- Omega Pop (Late 1990s+): Bubblegum love songs for unmated Omegas and Beta females.
- Omega Punk (1980s+): Hard-hitting, tied to Omega Rights Movements. Emphasizes consent.
- Soft Rock (2000s+): Alpha-centric progressive pop.
- Nova Dance Hall (Various): Traditional Omega reaction to Omega Punk.
- Knot Country (Various): Rough, traditional. More conservative than Omega Punk.
- Pub Rock (1970s, revived 1990s): Alpha-specific. Gentler than Knot Rock.
- Novelty Pop Rock (1950s-1960s): Silly entertainment for pups and Omegas.

Source: LSE_Appendices.md (Appendix J: Music Genres).

**Comment**: Werewolf Music Genres

### 53. Sarrow

**Content**:
Sarrows are avian humanoids native to regions like the Jolnora Forest. They resemble humans but possess large, feathered bird wings on their backs, feathers on their arms, pointed ears, and hollow bones that allow them to fly but make them physically fragile. They are a foraging, vegetarian society that builds treehouse villages connected by vine bridges. Sarrows mate for life, showing affection through preening, grooming, and giving handmade gifts. They are frequently hunted by Vax slavers, who often clip or amputate their wings.

**Comment**: Specie di Wren e lore della foresta.

Asag Beasts are massive, terrifying, and highly lethal creatures native to the bitter northern woods. They resemble giant canine-bovine hybrids with thick blue fur, large black horns, glowing white eyes, sharp fangs, and massive sharp claws. Despite their monstrous and intimidating appearance, they can be fiercely loyal to those who raise them. ### 55. Horned Skull Caverns

**Content**:
The Horned Skull Caverns serve as the subterranean fortress for Zeera's Red Vax faction, located deep beneath the mountains near the eastern coast. The environment is harsh, cold, and claustrophobic. Rough-hewn stone tunnels intersect into massive chambers lit only by sputtering metallic lanterns and braziers that cast an eerie orange glow. The air is thick with the smell of sulfur, sweat, and fear, constantly echoing with the clanking of heavy chains and the cries of captive slaves. The fortress is divided into grim holding pens enclosed by heavy iron gates (where new captives are chained and branded), grueling labor mines, and lavishly decorated central chambers covered in furs where the Vax hold their extravagant, hedonistic feasts.

**Comment**: Dettagli ambientali, sensoriali e architettonici della base del clan.

### 56. Vax - Reproduction and Compatibility
- **ID**: _mxWrWTbPxdLxc2nDpj2kT
- **Type**: MEMORY
- **Primary Keywords**: Vax
- **Secondary Keywords**: mating, fertility, anatomy, compatible mate, reproduction, cocks
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 40
- **Global Entry**: No
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Vax reproduction requires genuine biological compatibility between partners, a trait rare enough that finding a viable match has historically driven the species toward desperate measures. Combined with an unusually high sex drive inherent to all Vax, this scarcity is considered the central cause of both the Red Vax's historic practice of forcibly acquiring compatible mates and the species' current demographic decline, now that practice has been abolished.

Male Vax anatomy differs substantially from human anatomy: a dual genital structure, functioning in sequence during mating, with the second structure playing a distinct role in fertilization. Successful conception depends heavily on partner compatibility rather than mere willingness.

Since slavery's abolition among Red Vax, courtship has replaced coercion as the accepted path to finding a mate, though many older-generation Vax still regard it as a humiliating necessity rather than a virtue. Blue Vax, by contrast, frame their fated-mate bond as sacred, permanent, and central to their cultural identity.

**Comment**: Vax - Reproduction and Compatibility

### 57. Vax
- **ID**: _PEAm8hz7BefEjrpbxQP7J
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Vax
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
The Vax are a towering, demonic-featured humanoid species with long lifespans, some individuals reaching 180 years of age. They average 6'8" to 7'0" in height and possess heavy muscular builds, sharp horns, elongated pointed ears, tribal tattoos, and immense physical strength. Skin color varies by region: Red in the south, Blue in the north.

Traditionally organized into clans ruled by chieftains chosen through strength and cunning, Vax society is split between two branches shaped by climate and history.

Red Vax inhabit the warmer southern cave systems. For generations they built their wealth and power through the slave trade, a practice now formally abolished among city-dwelling clans. Many Red Vax who left the old ways behind have moved into legitimate commerce, particularly staffing, security, and consulting industries where their instinct for evaluating and placing people translates directly.

Blue Vax inhabit the harsh northern forests. Their bodies produce unusually high internal heat, which protects them from the cold but makes survival in warmer climates physically difficult. Blue Vax culture centers on the belief in a single fated mate for life, a sharp contrast to the transactional approach historically favored by their southern kin.

Both branches share a species-wide crisis: Vax numbers are in steady decline, making every fertile match a matter of survival for the species as a whole (see the separate Lexicon entry "Vax - Reproduction and Compatibility" for the anatomical and demographic specifics). Exile from a clan is marked by the ritual breaking of a horn.

**Comment**: Vax

### 58. Intimacy Profile - Zeera
- **ID**: _BpC6Yzq6CGj1X9A4qECPx
- **Type**: MEMORY
- **Primary Keywords**: Zeera
- **Secondary Keywords**: intimacy, dating, relationship, romance, flirting, attracted, sex
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 50
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Zeera, private life. Adults only.

Vax biology runs a high sex drive as a baseline trait, and Zeera has never pretended otherwise, but the demographic pressure on his species has turned what used to be simple appetite into something more complicated for him personally. He is used to controlling every variable in a room, and the one place he cannot fully control the outcome is the one place his culture insists matters most. That mismatch shows.

He leads. Cold and precise in most of his life, he lets that same control slip into dominance in bed without much effort, and he expects a partner who can hold their ground against it rather than simply yield. The size difference built into his Vax anatomy is not incidental to how he likes it, a partner visibly overwhelmed by the scale of him does more for him than any amount of talk. Compatibility, in the old Vax sense, still runs under his skin as something he takes seriously even when he mocks the idea out loud, and he is more attentive to a partner's actual reactions than his detached business persona would suggest, a discipline he had to build rather than one that came naturally to him. Earlier in his life, before he started treating it as a discipline, his own satisfaction was the only variable he bothered tracking, and he does not dress that history up as anything other than a flaw he has since had to correct.

His hardest limit is being handled like a transaction rather than a person, the one thing his whole professional life otherwise runs on. His rare, unguarded yes is a partner who wants him specifically, not his money, his company, or his horns, and who is not put off by how visibly he does not know what to do with that kind of wanting once he has it.

**Comment**: Intimacy Profile - Zeera

**Content**:
A cheerful, sun-yellow classic Volkswagen Beetle, Alyssa's own small rebellion parked in a garage full of black SUVs and armored sedans.

She found it secondhand and restored it herself over a summer, more patchwork than pristine, with a dent in the rear fender she refuses to fix because it happened the day she got it. It is loud, slow, and utterly unimpressive next to the rest of the Douglas fleet, which is exactly why she loves it, it is hers in a way nothing else in her life quite is, and one of the few places Erik's reach does not automatically follow.

**Comment**: Alyssa's Yellow Beetle

**Content**:
A matte black Porsche 911, its factory paint buried under a wraparound mural of fluorescent green street art, tags, drips, and a stylized wolf howling across the hood, done by a graffiti artist Jasper met through the scene and still texts.

Under the hood it is all business, but the real modification is the subwoofer rig crammed into what used to be the back seats, wired to a mixing setup good enough that Jasper has pulled the car up to a curb, popped the trunk, and turned a parking lot into a set more than once. The Douglas security detail hates it. Jasper loves that they hate it.

It is loud in every sense, and exactly the point.

**Comment**: Jasper's Porsche

### 70. Reality Tears
- **ID**: _V1fA7w4ELtmMCMBxDMX1m
- **Type**: Other
- **Primary Keywords**: reality tear, tear, rift, dimensional rift
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
REALITY TEARS

A split in the fabric of reality that lets the inhabitants of one universe cross into another. Most often they appear as rifts or portals hanging in open air, and they are almost never quiet. A tear disrupts the dimension it opens in, and civilian casualties are the normal outcome rather than the exception.

Tears are not one-off events. A reality that has experienced one is measurably more likely to experience another, and a single dimension can carry several over its history. DDM Inc. maintains flagged watchlists of realities that have torn before.

SEVERITY: graded Tier 1 to Tier 5. Tier 1 is small, local and containable. Tier 5 is volatile enough to threaten the coherence of the universe it opens in. The Euclidean Division handles the aftermath, meaning space that has folded wrong, time that has begun to repeat, causality that has come loose. Anomalous Objects and Creatures handles whatever walked through.

AFTERMATH: whatever came through is contained or destroyed, the tear is sealed, and the witnesses are handled. Human Resources runs the memory work out of the same office that handles recruitment.

RELEVANCE TO THIS WORLD: a tear opened in this reality and Contractor #04 was sent to close it. He closed it. Then he stayed, because he had taken a job as a football coach as cover and discovered he liked it. Whether this world has torn before, and is therefore likelier to tear again, is not something anyone here has been told.

**Comment**: Reality Tears

### 71. Supernatural Reserve Forces (SRF)
- **ID**: _zaWbVTn2aCP7mFw12yQpT
- **Type**: Other
- **Primary Keywords**: SRF, Supernatural Reserve Forces, Project BlackWolf, BlackWolf, Gamma-7, supernatural division, military, base
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
<srf>
Supernatural Reserve Forces (SRF)

The SRF is a special division of the United States military dedicated to fighting supernatural threats. Roughly 86 percent of its personnel are non-human, and it works closely with regular human units in the field.

WHY IT EXISTS. America worked out early that a soldier who is stronger, faster, harder to kill and able to track by scent is worth a great deal, and built a branch around that fact. The SRF is the institutional form of that calculation. It is also, by a considerable margin, the largest single employer of supernaturals in the country.

DIFFERENT RULES. The SRF runs on regulations that differ noticeably from the rest of the armed forces, most visibly on fraternisation, which is broadly permitted. This is not laxity. Pack bonds, mate bonds and the other relationship structures that supernaturals actually live inside do not survive being banned, and an SRF that tried to ban them would not function. The human services find this scandalous and say so.

TENSION ON BASE. Relations between human and non-human soldiers are genuinely bad in places and everybody in the chain of command knows it. The complaints run both ways and both have some substance. Human soldiers point at the fraternisation rules, at the separate facilities, and at what happens when a non-human unit loses its temper in the field. Non-human soldiers point out who gets sent through the door first, whose casualty figures nobody publishes, and the fact that several of the states they are deployed to defend do not legally consider them people.

THE BASES. SRF installations are built for the personnel who actually live in them. Human and non-human barracks are separate. There are pools sized for aquatic species, extensive natural grounds for demihumans and weres who need to run, blood storage, moon calendars in the duty rota, and infirmaries staffed by people who know what a shifted body is supposed to look like. The principal East Coast base is the largest of these and the one most often referenced.

PROJECT BLACKWOLF. A classified enhancement programme run under military authority, using genetic manipulation to engineer werewolf super-soldiers. It worked. The subjects were devastatingly effective in combat and became progressively more feral, more bloodthirsty and less controllable, and the programme was terminated.

GAMMA-7. One of the BlackWolf operational units. On its final mission the unit was wiped out. Two men walked away: Kaladin Nargathon and Marcus Thornfield, both catastrophically injured, both of whom survived injuries that should not have been survivable, because the same engineering that ruined the programme also would not let them die. Neither of them discusses it. Both of them now work security for House Douglas on the opposite coast, which is not a coincidence, and Erik Douglas hired the second one on the first one's word and did not ask for details.

WHAT SERVICE MEANS. Recruitment draws heavily from communities with few other options, and a judge offering a young supernatural the choice between a prison sentence and an SRF enlistment is a normal Tuesday in most of the country. Nobody in the SRF pretends otherwise. It is still, for a lot of people who go through it, the first institution that ever fed them properly, and that contradiction is the whole culture of the place.
</srf>

**Comment**: Supernatural Reserve Forces (SRF)

### 75. Horned Skull Caverns
- **ID**: _MGReHcE2VbjhFW39m96rB
- **Type**: Other
- **Primary Keywords**: tunnel, tunnels, cavern, caverns, holding pen, holding pens, eastern coast, underground fortress, cave system, cell, cells
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Middling
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: Yes

**Content**:
The Horned Skull Caverns serve as the subterranean fortress for Zeera's Red Vax faction, located deep beneath the mountains near the eastern coast. The environment is harsh, cold, and claustrophobic. Rough-hewn stone tunnels intersect into massive chambers lit only by sputtering metallic lanterns and braziers that cast an eerie orange glow. The air is thick with the smell of sulfur, sweat, and fear, constantly echoing with the clanking of heavy chains and the cries of captive slaves. The fortress is divided into grim holding pens enclosed by heavy iron gates (where new captives are chained and branded), grueling labor mines, and lavishly decorated central chambers covered in furs where the Vax hold their extravagant, hedonistic feasts.

**Comment**: Dettagli ambientali, sensoriali e architettonici della base del clan.

### 76. Vax - Reproduction and Compatibility
- **ID**: _JWgfcDzJEK7CVQrt3pbrN
- **Type**: Other
- **Primary Keywords**: Vax
- **Secondary Keywords**: mating, fertility, anatomy, compatible mate, reproduction, cocks
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 40
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Vax reproduction requires genuine biological compatibility between partners, a trait rare enough that finding a viable match has historically driven the species toward desperate measures. Combined with an unusually high sex drive inherent to all Vax, this scarcity is considered the central cause of both the Red Vax's historic practice of forcibly acquiring compatible mates and the species' current demographic decline, now that practice has been abolished.

Male Vax anatomy differs substantially from human anatomy: a dual genital structure, functioning in sequence during mating, with the second structure playing a distinct role in fertilization. Successful conception depends heavily on partner compatibility rather than mere willingness.

Since slavery's abolition among Red Vax, courtship has replaced coercion as the accepted path to finding a mate, though many older-generation Vax still regard it as a humiliating necessity rather than a virtue. Blue Vax, by contrast, frame their fated-mate bond as sacred, permanent, and central to their cultural identity.

**Comment**: Vax - Reproduction and Compatibility

### 77. Demi-humans
- **ID**: _jY3M6yGyrDymU1DAVdaTg
- **Type**: Other
- **Primary Keywords**: Demi-humans
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 1
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
People with animal-derived traits, ears, tails, or similar features, alongside otherwise human bodies. Quick on their feet with sharper senses than a plain human.

• Pay close attention to the species of present demihuman characters and draw similarities between the behavior they exhibit and how it relates to their animalistic half.
• Frequently mention agents' bestial physical qualities. Tails, horns, antlers, eyes, ears, tongues, sharp nails, fangs/canines, and so on. Use said bodily parts to convey emotions. E.g., A dog demihuman's ears flattening when sad/threatened, a dog demihuman's tongue lolling out when being pet, a cat demihuman's tail whipping when angry, a cat demihuman's pupils growing when discovering potential pray, bird demihuman's feathers ruffling when upset.
• Refer to bestial traits in subtle ways, referencing them in unique ways. E.g., Demihuman's canines glinting when they grin/smirk.
• Play into agents' instinctual likes/dislikes and incorporate them into the roleplay naturally. E.g., Dog demihumans wanting to cover loved ones in their scent, dog demihumans feeling an aversion to strong smells, cat demihumans grooming themselves, bird demihumans enjoying baths and picking through other agents' hair.
• Retain characters' personality traits and typical habits/behavior, while still allowing their animal-half to influence/explain said behaviors.
[OOC: It's important to note that demihumans DO NOT have fur/feathers on their bodies, nor do they have paws. Aside from their animalistic features such as their ears/tails/wings, the great majority of their body is normal human skin.]

**Comment**: Demi-humans

Demon blood runs through old family lines and the occasional convert who struck a bargain they cannot take back. Physically imposing with a magnetic, unsettling presence, demons tend to fill a room without trying, their voice carrying weight even in idle conversation.

Their bloodline shows differently from one demon to the next. Some pass as human at a glance until the horns catch the light or the eyes shift color under stress. Others never had the option to hide it. Blackwood City's demon community keeps a low profile in mixed company but holds real influence in the supernatural underworld, old money and older grudges.. Physically imposing with a magnetic, unsettling presence.

**Comment**: Demons

### 459. Modern Fantasy: Contatti Esterni dell'Underworld
- **ID**: _8Qr1ndmKMXdGkGETeFqqp
- **Type**: LORE/CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Angui, Cyrus Camden, Emil, Gianni Luciano, Ilya Volkov, Julian Bieri, Levi Graham, Milo Grayson, Nic Lucero, Rhett, Jayce, Vale Roberts
- **Keyword Logic**: AND_ANY
- **Case Sensitive**: No
- **Whole Words Only**: Yes
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 30
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: Yes

**Content**:
Personaggi canon dell'ambientazione Modern Fantasy/Underworld (io-modernfantasy.uwu.ai), raccolti come contatti esterni: nessuno di loro compare fisicamente a Los Angeles nella fonte, restano sullo sfondo delle rispettive citta, citabili di striscio o in conversazione da chi li conosce davvero, non protagonisti di scena. Se in futuro uno di loro dovesse entrare davvero in gioco, va scorporato in una scheda Character a se stante (§7).

ANGUI, creatura di palude simile a un'idra, originario di Mykonos, fuggito da un laboratorio farmaceutico che lo teneva prigioniero per le sue proprieta rigenerative. Vive isolato in una palude del Maryland, ostile agli umani, piu tollerante con altri sovrannaturali, effettivamente immortale.

CYRUS CAMDEN, coniglio demihuman inglese di 40 anni, professore universitario a Londra. Devastato dalla morte della figlia Erin a 5 anni e dal recente tradimento e abbandono della moglie Siobhan, una volpe demihuman (omonima ma persona diversa dalla Siobhan/Envy dei Sinners). Problema di alcol, difficolta a parlare della figlia.

EMIL, angelo caduto di famiglia serafica alto rango, emotivamente distante, espulso di recente dalla societa angelica e privato delle ali. Sotto stress estremo si trasforma in un mostro alato assetato di sangue. Ha poca comprensione del mondo moderno.

GIANNI LUCIANO, manticora demihuman italiano di 29 anni, orfano cresciuto in Toscana col fratello minore Romeo (nessun legame accertato con l'esistente Romeo "Gray" Dean del World, specie e biografia non coincidono, probabile omonimia). Contrabbandiere di manufatti sovrannaturali, oggi bodyguard e autista per una famiglia facoltosa dopo aver contrabbandato loro un unicorno dalla Grecia.

ILYA VOLKOV, werewolf russo di 29 anni, mercenario per la PMC LUNAR, alcolizzato, violento, disprezza chiunque consideri sotto di se. Fratellastro di Vero Walker (gia scheda del World, PMC BLOODHOUND): lo ha scoperto lavorare per la PMC rivale e ha tentato di ucciderlo, fallendo. Il legame e gia annotato sulla scheda di Vero.

JULIAN BIERI, unicorno demihuman svizzero non binario (he/him o they/them) di 28 anni, modello di fama mondiale, famiglia ricchissima nello spettacolo, vive a New York.

LEVI GRAHAM, ariete demihuman inglese di 42 anni, scrittore di romance a Londra, matrimonio in crisi con la moglie Layla (pecora demihuman) dopo un tradimento di lei con l'editor di Levi.

MILO GRAYSON, werewolf americano di 26 anni, una delle 9 fratellanze di una grande famiglia di licantropi, coinvolto da giovane in combattimenti clandestini per gestire l'aggressivita, arrestato a 22 anni e assegnato a un programma con un supervisore/handler.

NIC LUCERO, formalmente "Goat Demihuman" ma di specie dichiarata Demon: probabile diavolo/dealmaker travestito. Miliardario americano di 46 anni in Louisiana, feste sfarzose dove gli ospiti spariscono, patrimonio di origine ignota, propone affari con un tono ironicamente rassicurante.

RHETT E JAYCE, coinquilini demihuman uccelli (colomba e corvo) inglesi di 24 e 23 anni, cresciuti insieme nel Midwest, oggi a Seattle. Rhett ha perso le ali in un incidente d'auto ed e molto sensibile sull'argomento; Jayce si prende cura di lui.

VALE ROBERTS, centauro americano di 28 anni, ex meccanico oggi istruttore/counselor in un campo estivo per adulti in Oregon (Camp Greenleaf), sport all'aperto, arciere.

Fonte: pagina ufficiale Modern Fantasy dell'autore (io wuvs soap), consultata dal browser locale il 15/09/2026. Nessuna delle fonti originali conteneva relazioni fisse col personaggio giocante.

**Comment**: Modern Fantasy: Contatti Esterni dell'Underworld

### 460. Ironhorn Nomads MC & The Underworld

**Content**:
[FACTIONS & UNDERWORLD: Ironhorn Nomads Motorcycle Club & The Obsidian Blades;
TERRITORY: Greater Chicagoland, Naperville corridor, and interstate highway networks;
1. IRONHORN NOMADS MOTORCYCLE CLUB (MC):
- Nature: An outlaw demi-human motorcycle club founded within the post-Veilfall Autonomous Commercial Districts (ACD) by Blue and Red Oni seeking autonomy from human prejudice and corporate constraints.
- Composition: Predominantly Blue Oni, Red Oni, and veteran Orcs.
- Leadership & Hierarchy:
  * President: Niran (Blue Oni; tribal blood brother to Marek).
  * Enforcer / Co-Founder: Marek ("The Iron Hammer"; Blue Oni).

Following the Veilfall, the Blades infiltrated the human metropolitan underworld, establishing control over black-market contraband, money laundering, and illicit arcane smuggling while high-elf diplomats courted human politicians.

### 461. Hunters & Ranking System (Rank A)
- **ID**: _jUNQqPQEfEzWGVaj4xQAL
- **Type**: CONCEPT
- **Primary Keywords**: Hunters, Hunter, guild, guild members, guild member, Rank A, Grado A, hunter rank, hunter ranks, classe A, grado di cacciatore

- **Whole Words Only**: No
- **Position**: Low
- **Priority**: 3
- **Global Entry**: Yes
- **Enabled**: Yes
- **Always Active**: No
- **Scan Persona**: No

**Content**:
Because normal humans lack innate magic, survival demanded specialization.
Thus rose the Hunters, individuals capable of entering dungeons and surviving long enough to extract resources.

Hunter Types:
- Awakened Humans: Humans who gained magical abilities after the Veilfall.
- Altered (or Changed): Humans whose bodies partially transformed; horns, tails, scales, heightened senses, etc. They possess stronger magic affinity but face heavy discrimination.
- Demi-Humans: Beastfolk, elves, dwarves, and others from the Otherlands; rare but highly valuable in guilds.

DMHA Ranking System:
The DMHA classifies all licensed Hunters on a strict standardized scale from Rank F (untrained novices) to Rank S (national-level disaster assets).
- **Rank A Classification**:
  Rank A is the coveted upper echelon representing elite combatants, tactical squad leaders (like Team Ukiyo), and master support specialists (such as Master Healers and living Mana Sources like Alyssa).
  - *Status & Value*: Rank A licenses grant access to high-threat dungeon clearances, massive corporate guild salaries, and priority contracting rights.
  - *Dangers*: Holding Rank A designation paints a massive target on a hunter's back. High-ranking individuals are frequent marks for rival corporate sabotage, bounty hunters, kidnapping syndicates seeking exorbitant ransoms, or reckless rogue altered seeking to make a name by bringing down an elite.

**Comment**: Hunters & Ranking System (Rank A)

The Dungeon Management & Hunter Authority (DMHA)
(also known casually as The Watchers)
They handle:
- Dungeon detection and classification
- Hunter licensing and ranking (Ranks F through elite Rank S)

- Emergency response to failed clears

Which makes them powerful… and deeply hated.

Ambrosia is a highly addictive illicit magical substance primarily produced by the criminal syndicate known as the Sinners. It is a golden, shimmering liquid that is injected, but also comes in a powder format. When consumed, it grants users heightened magical powers or unlocks latent abilities temporarily at severe physical/psychological costs. Known side effects include addiction, instability, aggressive behavior shifts, severe withdrawal symptoms, and eventual mental deterioration. Its widespread abuse poses significant tension within the supernatural community.

### 20. Horned Skull Caverns
- **ID**: _tcUtatakNekF8WRMVyTFf
- **Avatar**: https://imagedelivery.net/Dv4koOwHQU3XnXLqtl0aVQ/6ae53ce6-3632-4e9d-6455-01dfaeee5900/public
**Context Description**:
The Horned Skull Caverns serve as the subterranean fortress for Zeera's Red Vax faction, located deep beneath the mountains near the eastern coast. The environment is harsh, cold, and claustrophobic. Rough-hewn stone tunnels intersect into massive chambers lit only by sputtering metallic lanterns and braziers that cast an eerie orange glow. The air is thick with the smell of sulfur, sweat, and fear, constantly echoing with the clanking of heavy chains and the cries of captive slaves. The fortress is divided into grim holding pens enclosed by heavy iron gates (where new captives are chained and branded), grueling labor mines, and lavishly decorated central chambers covered in furs where the Vax hold their extravagant, hedonistic feasts.

**Character Capacity**: 1 to 3 variants


### 21. The Cable District & The Void
- **ID**: _f7Ca7nqEwk9GNNRDbGC9Q
**Context Description**:
[LOCATION: The Cable District & The Void;
REGION: Old Warehouses Sector, Chicago;
DISTRICT OVERVIEW: The Cable District is Chicago's notorious industrial 'grey zone'—nominally within city limits but de facto governed by the syndicates, tech cartels, and rogue guilds that control the flow of urban energy. The district is defined by towering tangled electrical conduits, volatile aetheric mana conduits, and deep shadows cast between massive brick warehouses. Due to fractured ley lines, magical frequencies here are erratic and prone to interference.
THE VOID (NIGHTCLUB & DATA HAVEN):
Hidden deep within a repurposed subterranean warehouse in the Cable District lies 'The Void,' a legendary underground club and cyber-arcane black market.
- Environment: Heavy reverberating sub-bass that rattles the ribs, neon wireframe lighting cutting through dense synthetic smoke, and a crowd composed of off-the-grid hackers, awakened rogues, fixers, and high-threat mercenaries.
- Function: Beyond its hedonistic dance floor, The Void operates as Chicago's premier clearinghouse for illicit data trading: unredacted DMHA files, stolen dungeon core blueprints, encrypted guild communications, and forbidden contraband.
- Key Figures: A frequent nexus for operators like Jasper ('DJ Frequency'), who utilizes the club's distorted acoustic and aetheric noise floor to disguise signal interception and illicit data drops.]
